# Lecturas del portal resueltas a PeopleNet

Generado por `scripts/portal/resolver-tablas.ts` solo con el diccionario Meta4
(`M4RCH_*` / `M4RDC_*`). Cada nodo muestra su sentencia de lectura original y
los conectores de campo con su nodo padre. `&OBJ` es un objeto lógico: su
tabla física está en la tabla final.

## CSP_CERT_DOC!CSP_CERT_DOC

- TI: `CSP_CERT_DOC` (raíz) · objeto de lectura: `CSP_CERT_DOC` → `M4CSP_CERT_DOC`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_OR_DOC = A.SCO_OR_DOC, @SCO_NM_DOC = A.SCO_NM_DOC, @SCO_CERT_DOC = A.SCO_CERT_DOC, @ANIO = A.ANIO, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT FROM &CSP_CERT_DOC A ORDER BY A.ANIO ASC
```

## CSP_CERTIFICADO_HAB_ANIOS!CSP_CERTIFICADO_HAB_ANIOS

- TI: `CSP_CERTIFICADO_HAB_ANIOS` (raíz) · objeto de lectura: `STD_HR_PERIOD` → `STD_HR_PERIOD`

```sql
SELECT @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = A.STD_DT_END FROM &STD_HR_PERIOD A
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_CERTIFICADOS_CV

- TI: `CSP_CERTIFICADOS_CV` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_CERTIFICATION_LICENSE` → `M4SCO_CERTIF_LICEN`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_CERTIF_LIC = A.SCO_OR_CERTIF_LIC, @SCO_DT_ISSUE = A.SCO_DT_ISSUE, @SCO_DT_EXPIRED = A.SCO_DT_EXPIRED, @SCO_N_CERTIF = A.SCO_N_CERTIF, @SCO_ID_CERTIF_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_CERTIF_TYPE, @SCO_ID_ISSUE_ENTIT = A.SCO_ID_ISSUE_ENTIT, @SCO_CERT_NUMBER = A.SCO_CERT_NUMBER, @SCO_ID_COUNTRY = A.SCO_ID_COUNTRY, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT FROM &SCO_CERTIFICATION_LICENSE A WHERE (A.SCO_DT_EXPIRED >= #TODAY ())
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_CV

- TI: `CSP_CV` (raíz) · objeto de lectura: `STD_HR_PERIOD` → `STD_HR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 1: `P_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SCO_BLOB_PHOTO = C.SCO_BLOB_PHOTO, @SSP_NM_CATEGORIA = L.SSP_NM_CATEGORIA, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = G.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_DT_START = F.SCO_DT_START, @SSP_ID_CATEGORIA = H.SSP_ID_CATEGORIA, @SSP_FEC_ANTIGUEDAD = A.SSP_FEC_ANTIGUEDAD, @STD_DT_BIRTH = C.STD_DT_BIRTH, @CSP_ID_WORK_FUNCTIONAL = I.CSP_ID_WORK_FUNCTIONAL, @CSP_N_WORK_FUNCTIONAL = K.CSP_N_WORK_FUNCTIONAL, @STD_N_FIRST_NAME = C.STD_N_FIRST_NAME, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = C.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @STD_EMAIL = J.STD_EMAIL FROM &STD_HR_PERIOD A, STD_PERSON C, SCO_HR_ROLE E, SCO_H_HR_JOB F, STD_JOB G, SSP_H_CATEGORIA H, SCO_H_HR_WLOCATION I, STD_EMAIL J, CSP_WORK_FUNCTIONAL K, SSP_CATEGORIAS L WHERE E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND E.SCO_ID_HR(+) = A.STD_ID_HR AND E.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD AND A.STD_ID_HR = C.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND J.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = C.ID_ORGANIZATION AND J.STD_ID_PERSON(+) = C.STD_ID_PERSON AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = E.ID_ORGANIZATION AND F.SCO_ID_HR(+) = E.SCO_ID_HR AND F.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = E.ID_ORGANIZATION AND H.SCO_ID_HR(+) = E.SCO_ID_HR AND H.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = E.ID_ORGANIZATION AND I.SCO_ID_HR(+) = E.SCO_ID_HR AND I.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = G.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND I.CSP_ID_WORK_FUNCTIONAL = K.CSP_ID_WORK_FUNCTIONAL(+) AND I.ID_ORGANIZATION = K.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION = L.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND H.SSP_ID_CATEGORIA = L.SSP_ID_CATEGORIA(+) AND (A.STD_ID_HR = @P_ID_HR And E.SCO_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) And F.SCO_DT_END (+) = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) And H.SSP_FEC_FIN (+) = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) And I.SCO_DT_END (+) = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) And J.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = '2' And J.STD_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) And F.SCO_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) And A.STD_ID_HR_TYPE = '01')
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_EXPERIENCIA_PROF_EXTERNA

- TI: `CSP_EXPERIENCIA_PROF_EXTERNA` · objeto de lectura: `STD_HR_PREV_JOBS` → `STD_HR_PREV_JOBS`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_N_COUNTRY = C.STD_N_COUNTRY, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = A.STD_DT_END, @STD_EMPLOYER = A.STD_EMPLOYER, @STD_N_SECTOR = B.STD_N_SECTOR, @STD_INITIAL_JOB = A.STD_INITIAL_JOB FROM &STD_HR_PREV_JOBS A, STD_LU_JOB_SECTOR B, STD_COUNTRY C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_SECTOR = B.STD_ID_SECTOR(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = C.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND (A.CSP_EXP_INT = '0') ORDER BY A.STD_DT_START ASC
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_EXPERIENCIA_PROF_INTERNA

- TI: `CSP_EXPERIENCIA_PROF_INTERNA` · objeto de lectura: `STD_HR_PREV_JOBS` → `STD_HR_PREV_JOBS`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = A.STD_DT_END, @CSP_N_PREV_JOB = A.CSP_N_PREV_JOB FROM &STD_HR_PREV_JOBS A WHERE ((   A.CSP_EXP_INT = '1'  )) ORDER BY A.STD_DT_END ASC, A.STD_DT_START ASC
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_FORMACION_EXTERNA

- TI: `CSP_FORMACION_EXTERNA` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_HR_COMP_BACKGROUND` → `M4SCO_HR_COMP_BACK`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_N_COURSE = A.SCO_N_COURSE, @SCO_NUMBER_HOURS = A.SCO_NUMBER_HOURS, @SCO_N_CENTER = A.SCO_N_CENTER FROM &SCO_HR_COMP_BACKGROUND A WHERE (A.CSP_INTERNA IS NULL Or A.CSP_INTERNA = '0') ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START ASC
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_FORMACION_INTERNA

- TI: `CSP_FORMACION_INTERNA` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_HR_COMP_BACKGROUND` → `M4SCO_HR_COMP_BACK`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SCO_N_COURSE = A.SCO_N_COURSE, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_N_CENTER = A.SCO_N_CENTER, @SCO_NUMBER_HOURS = A.SCO_NUMBER_HOURS FROM &SCO_HR_COMP_BACKGROUND A WHERE (A.CSP_INTERNA = '1') ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START ASC
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_IDIOMAS

- TI: `CSP_IDIOMAS` · objeto de lectura: `STD_HT_HR_LANG` → `STD_HT_HR_LANG`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @CSP_TOEIC = A.CSP_TOEIC, @CSP_FECHA_TOEIC = A.CSP_FECHA_TOEIC, @STD_N_LANGUAGE = B.STD_N_LANGUAGE, @STD_ID_LANGUAGE = A.STD_ID_LANGUAGE, @STD_N_LANG_LEVEL = C.STD_N_LANG_LEVEL FROM &STD_HT_HR_LANG A, STD_LU_LANGUAGES B, STD_LU_LANG_LEVEL C WHERE A.STD_ID_LANGUAGE = B.STD_ID_LANGUAGE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_LISTEN_LEVEL = C.STD_ID_LANG_LEVEL(+) AND (A.STD_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ))
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_OTROS_TITULOS

- TI: `CSP_OTROS_TITULOS_CV` · objeto de lectura: `CSP_OTROS_TITULOS` → `M4CSP_OTROS_TITULOS`
- Conector: `ID_ORGANIZATION` = `CSP_CV.ID_ORGANIZATION`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @CSP_TITULO = A.CSP_TITULO, @DT_END = A.DT_END FROM &CSP_OTROS_TITULOS A ORDER BY A.DT_END ASC
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_PARAM_GLOBAL

- TI: `CSP_PARAM_GLOBAL` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_PARAM_GLOBALES` → `M4SSE_PARAM_GLOBALES`
- Parámetro 1: `P_PARAMETRO`

```sql
SELECT @PARAMETRO = A.PARAMETRO, @VALOR = A.VALOR FROM &SSE_PARAM_GLOBALES A WHERE (A.PARAMETRO = @P_PARAMETRO)
```

## CSP_CV!CSP_TITULACION

- TI: `CSP_TITULACION` · objeto de lectura: `STD_HR_ACAD_BACKGROUND` → `STD_HR_ACAD_BACKGR`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `CSP_CV.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_ORD_ACD_BACK = A.STD_ORD_ACD_BACK, @STD_ID_SECTOR = A.STD_ID_SECTOR, @STD_ID_EDU_TYPE = A.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_EARNED_EXPE = A.STD_DT_EARNED_EXPE, @STD_ID_DIPLOMA = A.STD_ID_DIPLOMA, @STD_ID_EDU_CENTER = A.STD_ID_EDU_CENTER, @STD_COMMENT = A.STD_COMMENT, @STD_TOTAL_YEARS_DIP = A.STD_TOTAL_YEARS_DIP, @STD_COMPLETED_YEARS = A.STD_COMPLETED_YEARS, @SCO_CK_END_DIP = A.SCO_CK_END_DIP, @STD_DESC_EDU_CENTER = A.STD_DESC_EDU_CENTER, @STD_ID_EDU_SP = A.STD_ID_EDU_SP, @STD_N_EDU_TYPE = B.STD_N_EDU_TYPE, @STD_N_EDU_SP = C.STD_N_EDU_SP, @STD_N_EXT_ORG = E.STD_N_EXT_ORG, @STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = F.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_COUNTRY = G.STD_N_COUNTRY FROM &STD_HR_ACAD_BACKGROUND A, STD_LU_EDU_TYPE B, STD_LU_EDU_SPECIALITY C, STD_EXTERNAL_ORGANIZATION E, SCO_EDUC_CENTER D, STD_COUNTRY G, STD_ADDRESS F WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE = B.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_SP = C.STD_ID_EDU_SP(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE = C.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND D.SCO_ID_EDUC_CENTER = E.STD_ID_EXTERNAL_ORG(+) AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = E.ID_ORGANIZATION AND F.STD_ID_EXTERNAL_ORG(+) = E.STD_ID_EXTERNAL_ORG AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_CENTER = D.SCO_ID_EDUC_CENTER(+) AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.STD_ID_COUNTRY = G.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) ORDER BY A.STD_ID_DIPLOMA DESC
```

## CSP_MNG_DIPLOMAS_PORTAL!CSP_FREE_CALENDARIO_SESION

- TI: `CSP_NS_FREE_CALENDARIO_SE_1` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_SUBACTION_CALENDAR` → `M4SCO_SUBACTION_CA`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_P_ID_SESION`
- Parámetro 2: `CSP_P_ID_SOCIEDAD`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, @SCO_OR_CALENDAR = A.SCO_OR_CALENDAR, @SCO_DATE = A.SCO_DATE FROM &SCO_SUBACTION_CALENDAR A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = @CSP_P_ID_SESION And A.ID_ORGANIZATION = @CSP_P_ID_SOCIEDAD) ORDER BY A.SCO_DATE ASC
```

## CSP_MNG_DIPLOMAS_PORTAL!CSP_FREE_EMPRESA

- TI: `CSP_NS_FREE_EMPRESA_1` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_HR_LEGAL_ENTITY` → `M4SCO_H_HR_LEGENT`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_P_ID_HR`
- Parámetro 2: `CSP_P_FECHA`
- Parámetro 3: `CSP_P_FECHA`
- Parámetro 4: `CSP_P_ID_SOCIEDAD`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_ID_LEG_ENT = A.SCO_ID_LEG_ENT, @STD_N_LEG_ENT = C.STD_N_LEG_ENT, @SSP_ID_LEGAL = C.SSP_ID_LEGAL, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION FROM &SCO_H_HR_LEGAL_ENTITY A, STD_LEG_ENT C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEG_ENT = C.STD_ID_LEG_ENT(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_HR = @CSP_P_ID_HR And A.DT_START <= @CSP_P_FECHA And A.DT_END >= @CSP_P_FECHA And C.SSP_ID_TP_ID_LGAL = 'F' And A.ID_ORGANIZATION = @CSP_P_ID_SOCIEDAD)
```

## CSP_MNG_DIPLOMAS_PORTAL!CSP_MNG_DIPLOMAS_PORTAL

- TI: `CSP_NS_MNG_DIPLOMAS_PORTA_1` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_ENROLLMENT` → `M4SCO_ENROLLMENT`
- Parámetro 1: `P_PERSONA`
- Parámetro 2: `P_ID_DEV_SUBACTION`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION = B.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = D.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @STD_SSN = D.STD_SSN, @STD_N_FIRST_NAME = D.STD_N_FIRST_NAME, @SCO_HOURS = A.SCO_HOURS, @SCO_HOURS_OTW = A.SCO_HOURS_OTW, @SFR_CK_DEDUCTIBLE = B.SFR_CK_DEDUCTIBLE FROM &SCO_ENROLLMENT A, SCO_SUBACTION B, STD_PERSON D WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_PERSON = D.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND ((   A.SCO_DT_CANCEL Is Null   And   A.SCO_ID_NON_ATT_REASON Is Null   And   D.SSP_ID_TP_DOC In ( '1' , '7' )   And   A.STD_ID_PERSON = @P_PERSONA   And   @P_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION  ))
```

## CSP_MNG_DIPLOMAS_PORTAL!CSP_NODO_RAIZ_PORTAL

- TI: `CSP_NODO_RAIZ_PORTAL` (raíz) · objeto de lectura: `ORO_EMPLEADOS` → `M4ORO_EMPLEADOS`
- Parámetro 1: `P_PERSONA`

```sql
SELECT @ID_EMPLEADO = A.ID_EMPLEADO FROM &ORO_EMPLEADOS A WHERE (A.ID_EMPLEADO = @P_PERSONA)
```

## CSP_MNG_DIPLOMAS_PORTAL!CSP_SESION_PORTAL

- TI: `CSP_NS_SESION_PORTAL_1` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_SUBACTION` → `M4SCO_SUBACTION`
- Parámetro 1: `P_SESION`
- Parámetro 2: `P_SOCIEDAD`

```sql
SELECT @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, @SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE = B.SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE, @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION = A.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @SSP_SUBACT_DESCRIP = A.SSP_SUBACT_DESCRIP, @SCO_P_DAYS_HOURS = A.SCO_P_DAYS_HOURS, @SSP_ID_FEFE_SUBACT = A.SSP_ID_FEFE_SUBACT, @SCO_DAYS = A.SCO_DAYS, @SSP_ID_FEFE = A.SSP_ID_FEFE, @SCO_ID_DEV_PRO_TYPE = C.SCO_ID_DEV_PRO_TYPE, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @SCO_HOURS = A.SCO_HOURS, @SCO_HOURS_OTW = A.SCO_HOURS_OTW, @SFR_CK_DEDUCTIBLE = A.SFR_CK_DEDUCTIBLE FROM &SCO_SUBACTION A, SCO_X_DEV_ACT_TYPE B, SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE = B.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = @P_SESION And @P_SOCIEDAD = A.ID_ORGANIZATION)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_ASISTENTES_VAL

- TI: `CSP_NS_ASISTENTES_VAL` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT` → `M4SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRO`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, @STD_ID_PERSON = C.STD_ID_PERSON, @SCO_DT_ENROLL = C.SCO_DT_ENROLL, @SCO_DT_CANCEL = C.SCO_DT_CANCEL, @SCO_GB_NAME = D.SCO_GB_NAME FROM &SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT A, SCO_SUBACTION B, SCO_ENROLLMENT C, STD_PERSON D WHERE B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = B.ID_ORGANIZATION AND C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) = B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_PERSON = D.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = @CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT And B.SCO_ID_SUBACTION_STATUS = '02' And C.STD_ID_PERSON IS NOT NULL And D.STD_ID_PERSON IS NOT NULL)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_FREE_CAL_WU_EMPL

- TI: `CSP_NS_FREE_CAL_WU_EMPL` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_HR_WUNIT` → `M4SCO_H_HR_WUNIT`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_P_ID_EMPL`
- Parámetro 2: `CSP_P_FECHA`
- Parámetro 3: `CSP_P_FECHA`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT FROM &SCO_H_HR_WUNIT A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_HR = @CSP_P_ID_EMPL And A.SCO_DT_START <= @CSP_P_FECHA And A.SCO_DT_END >= @CSP_P_FECHA)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_FREE_CUERPO_MAIL

- TI: `CSP_NS_FREE_CUERPO_MAIL` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_MAIL_WF` → `M4SCO_MAIL_WF`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_P_ID_MAIL`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_MAIL = A.SCO_ID_MAIL, @SCO_N_TEXT = A.SCO_N_TEXT, @SCO_SUBJECT = A.SCO_SUBJECT, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT FROM &SCO_MAIL_WF A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_MAIL = @CSP_P_ID_MAIL)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_FREE_EV_EVAL_ANSW

- TI: `CSP_NS_FREE_EV_EVAL_ANSW` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT` → `M4SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRO`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, @SCO_ID_ANSWER_TYPE = D.SCO_ID_ANSWER_TYPE, @SCO_ID_ANSWER_VALUE = D.SCO_ID_ANSWER_VALUE, @SCO_SHORT_ANSWER = D.SCO_SHORT_ANSWER FROM &SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT A, SCO_SUBACTION B, SCO_EVENT_EVAL_ANSWER D WHERE B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT AND B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = D.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) AND (B.SCO_DT_CANCEL IS NULL And D.SCO_SHORT_ANSWER IS NULL And A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = @CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT And D.SCO_ID_ANSWER_TYPE IS NOT NULL And B.SCO_ID_SUBACTION_STATUS = '02' And D.SCO_ID_ANSWER_TYPE <> '02' And D.SCO_ID_QUESTION = '0024' And D.SCO_ID_ANSWER_VALUE <> '00')
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_FREE_PARAM_GLOBALES

- TI: `CSP_NS_FREE_PARAM_GLOBALES` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_PARAM_GLOBALES` → `M4SSE_PARAM_GLOBALES`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_P_PARAMETRO`

```sql
SELECT @PARAMETRO = A.PARAMETRO, @VALOR = A.VALOR FROM &SSE_PARAM_GLOBALES A WHERE (A.PARAMETRO = @CSP_P_PARAMETRO)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_FREE_VAL_EFI_ESS

- TI: `CSP_FREE_VAL_EFI_ESS` · objeto de lectura: `CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA` → `M4CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA`

```sql
SELECT @CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @CSP_ID_ANSWER_TYPE = A.CSP_ID_ANSWER_TYPE, @CSP_LISTA_ASISTENTES = A.CSP_LISTA_ASISTENTES, @CSP_PARTICIPANTES_PREVISTOS = A.CSP_PARTICIPANTES_PREVISTOS, @CSP_ASISTENTES = A.CSP_ASISTENTES, @CSP_HORAS_PLANIFICADAS = A.CSP_HORAS_PLANIFICADAS, @CSP_HORAS_REALIZADAS = A.CSP_HORAS_REALIZADAS, @CSP_VALORA_MEDIA_ASISTENTES = A.CSP_VALORA_MEDIA_ASISTENTES, @CSP_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = B.CSP_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @DT_START = B.DT_START, @DT_END = B.DT_END, @SCO_EDUCAT_OBJ = C.SCO_EDUCAT_OBJ FROM &CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA A, CSP_CURSOS_CERRADOS B, SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT C WHERE A.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = B.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_FREE_VAL_EFI_MSS

- TI: `CSP_FREE_VAL_EFI_MSS` · objeto de lectura: `CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA` → `M4CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA`

```sql
SELECT @CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @CSP_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = B.CSP_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @CSP_ACCIONES_DESEMPENADAS = A.CSP_ACCIONES_DESEMPENADAS, @CSP_OBSERVACIONES = A.CSP_OBSERVACIONES, @CSP_ID_ANSWER_VALUE = A.CSP_ID_ANSWER_VALUE, @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG FROM &CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA A, CSP_CURSOS_CERRADOS B WHERE A.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = B.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_FREE_VALORACION_EFICACIA

- TI: `CSP_NS_FREE_VALOR_EFICACIA` · objeto de lectura: `CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA` → `M4CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT`
- Parámetro 2: `CSP_P_ID_PERSONA`

```sql
SELECT @CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT_1 = A.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @CSP_ID_WORK_UNIT_RESP = A.CSP_ID_WORK_UNIT_RESP, @CSP_PARTICIPANTES_PREVISTOS = A.CSP_PARTICIPANTES_PREVISTOS, @CSP_ASISTENTES = A.CSP_ASISTENTES, @CSP_HORAS_PLANIFICADAS = A.CSP_HORAS_PLANIFICADAS, @CSP_HORAS_REALIZADAS = A.CSP_HORAS_REALIZADAS, @CSP_VALORA_MEDIA_ASISTENTES = A.CSP_VALORA_MEDIA_ASISTENTES, @CSP_ACCIONES_DESEMPENADAS = A.CSP_ACCIONES_DESEMPENADAS, @CSP_OBSERVACIONES = A.CSP_OBSERVACIONES, @CSP_ID_ANSWER_TYPE = A.CSP_ID_ANSWER_TYPE, @CSP_ID_ANSWER_VALUE = A.CSP_ID_ANSWER_VALUE, @ORDINAL = A.ORDINAL, @NIVEL_ACEPTADO = A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO, @CSP_LISTA_ASISTENTES = A.CSP_LISTA_ASISTENTES, @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE FROM &CSP_VALORACION_EFICACIA A WHERE (A.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = @CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT And A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = @CSP_P_ID_PERSONA)
```

## CSP_MNG_VALORA_EFICA!CSP_HORAS_ASISTENCIA

- TI: `CSP_NS_HORAS_ASISTENCIA` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_SUBACTION` → `M4SCO_SUBACTION`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT` = `CSP_ASISTENTES_VAL.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @CSP_HORAS_CAL = #SUM(B.CSP_HORAS_CAL), @CSP_HORAS = #SUM(B.CSP_HORAS) FROM &SCO_SUBACTION A, CSP_ASISTENCIA B WHERE A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) AND ((   A.SCO_DT_CANCEL Is Null   And   B.CSP_MES <> 0.0  )) GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT
```

```sql
SELECT @CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.CSP_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @CSP_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.CSP_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END FROM &CSP_CURSOS_CERRADOS A
```



## CSP_PUBLICACIONES_ESS!CSP_PUBLICACIONES_ESS

- TI: `CSP_PUBLICACIONES_ESS` (raíz) · objeto de lectura: `SCH_TEMPL_DOC` → `M4RCH_TEMPL_DOC`

```sql
SELECT @ID_FILE = A.ID_FILE, @ID_TEMPLATE = A.ID_TEMPLATE, @N_FILE = A.N_FILE, @DOC_CONTENT = A.DOC_CONTENT, @ID_CATEGORY = C.ID_CATEGORY, @ID_SUBCATEGORY = C.ID_SUBCATEGORY, @N_SUBCATEGORY = C.N_SUBCATEGORY, @N_CATEGORY = D.N_CATEGORY FROM &SCH_TEMPL_DOC A, SRP_LU_CATEGORIES D, SRP_LU_SUBCATEGORIES C, SCH_TEMPLATE_FILES B WHERE C.ID_CATEGORY = D.ID_CATEGORY(+) AND B.ID_CATEGORY = C.ID_CATEGORY(+) AND B.ID_SUBCATEGORY = C.ID_SUBCATEGORY(+) AND A.ID_TEMPLATE = B.ID_TEMPLATE(+) AND (A.ID_TEMPLATE = 'PUBLICACIONESESS' And C.ID_CATEGORY = 'DOCRRHH' And C.ID_SUBCATEGORY = 'RRHHESS')
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_CENTROS

- TI: `CSP_CENTROS` · objeto de lectura: `STD_WORK_LOCATION` → `STD_WORK_LOCATION`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION = A.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION, @STD_N_WORK_LOCATION = A.STD_N_WORK_LOCATION FROM &STD_WORK_LOCATION A ORDER BY A.STD_N_WORK_LOCATION ASC
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_CENTROS_FUN

- TI: `CSP_CENTROS_FUN` · objeto de lectura: `ORO_EMPLEADOS` → `M4ORO_EMPLEADOS`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @ID_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.ID_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @N_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.N_CENTRO_TRABAJO FROM &ORO_EMPLEADOS A ORDER BY A.N_CENTRO_TRABAJO ASC
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_CUENTA_BANCARIA_EMPLEADO

- TI: `CSP_CUENTA_BANCARIA_EMPLEADO` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_PERSON_BANK` → `M4SCO_PERSON_BANK`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_PERSON` = `CSP_DATOS_PAGO_EMPLEADO.SCO_ID_HR`
- Conector: `SCO_OR_ACCOUNT` = `CSP_DATOS_PAGO_EMPLEADO.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_OR_ACCOUNT = A.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT, @SCO_ID_PERSON = A.SCO_ID_PERSON, @SCO_GB_BANK = A.SCO_GB_BANK, @SCO_GB_IBAN = A.SCO_GB_IBAN FROM &SCO_PERSON_BANK A
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_CUENTA_BENEFICIARIO

- TI: `CSP_CUENTA_BENEFICIARIO` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_PERSON_BANK` → `M4SCO_PERSON_BANK`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_PERSON` = `CSP_DATOS_PAGO_EMPLEADO.SCO_ID_HR`
- Conector: `SCO_OR_ACCOUNT` = `CSP_DATOS_PAGO_EMPLEADO.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_PERSON = A.SCO_ID_PERSON, @SCO_OR_ACCOUNT = A.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT, @SCO_GB_IBAN = A.SCO_GB_IBAN FROM &SCO_PERSON_BANK A WHERE (A.SCO_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_DATOS_PAGO_EMPLEADO

- TI: `CSP_DATOS_PAGO_EMPLEADO` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_PAYMENT_DATA` → `M4SCO_PAYMENT_DATA`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR` = `CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA.ID_EMPLEADO`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA = A.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA, @SCO_OR_ACCOUNT = A.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT FROM &SCO_PAYMENT_DATA A WHERE (( A.SCO_EMP_CHECK = '1' And A.SCO_DT_END = #DATE ( '4000-01-01' )))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_DIRECCION_EMPLEADO

- TI: `CSP_DIRECCION_EMPLEADO` · objeto de lectura: `STD_ADDRESS` → `STD_ADDRESS`
- Conector: `STD_ID_PERSON` = `CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA.ID_EMPLEADO`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_GB_ADDRESS = A.SCO_GB_ADDRESS, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON FROM &STD_ADDRESS A WHERE ((   A.STD_DT_END = #DATE('4000-01-01')   And   A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE In ( '1' , '8' )  ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_EXCP_VISIBILIDAD_ORO

- TI: `CSP_EXCP_VISIBILIDAD_ORO` · objeto de lectura: `CSP_EXCP_VISIBILIDAD_ORO` → `M4CSP_EXCP_VISIBILIDAD_ORO`

```sql
SELECT @ID_TIPO = A.ID_TIPO, @ID_UNIDAD_NV = A.ID_UNIDAD_NV, @ID_UNIDAD = A.ID_UNIDAD FROM &CSP_EXCP_VISIBILIDAD_ORO A
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_FAM_IRPF

- TI: `CSP_FAM_IRPF` · objeto de lectura: `CSP_FAM_EMPLEADOS` → `M4CSP_FAM_EMPLEADOS`
- Conector: `STD_ID_PERSON` = `CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA.ID_EMPLEADO`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_DT_BIRTH = A.STD_DT_BIRTH, @NOMBRECOMPLETO = (#CONCAT_GENERIC (A.STD_N_FIRST_NAME , ' ' , A.SSP_PRIMER_APELLIDO , '  ' , A.STD_N_MAIDEN_NAME )), @STD_N_ACT_DEP_TYPE = B.STD_N_ACT_DEP_TYPE FROM &CSP_FAM_EMPLEADOS A, STD_LU_ACT_DEP_TYPE B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE = B.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE(+) ORDER BY A.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE ASC
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_FICHA

- TI: `CSP_FICHA` · objeto de lectura: `ORO_EMPLEADOS` → `M4ORO_EMPLEADOS`

```sql
SELECT @ID_EMPLEADO = A.ID_EMPLEADO, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @NOMBRE_COMPLETO = (A.NOMBRE + ' ' + A.APELLIDO_1 + ' ' + A.APELLIDO_2), @SCO_BLOB_PHOTO = B.SCO_BLOB_PHOTO, @ID_UNIDAD_RAIZ = A.ID_UNIDAD_RAIZ, @N_PUESTO = A.N_PUESTO, @FEC_ALTA_EMPLEADO = A.FEC_ALTA_EMPLEADO, @APELLIDO_1 = A.APELLIDO_1, @APELLIDO_2 = A.APELLIDO_2, @NOMBRE = A.NOMBRE, @CORREO = A.CORREO, @N_UNIDAD_RAIZ = A.N_UNIDAD_RAIZ, @ID_DIRECCION = A.ID_DIRECCION, @N_DIRECCION = A.N_DIRECCION, @ID_TIPO_DIRECCION = A.ID_TIPO_DIRECCION, @ID_AREA = A.ID_AREA, @N_AREA = A.N_AREA, @ID_TIPO_AREA = A.ID_TIPO_AREA, @ID_UNIDAD = A.ID_UNIDAD, @N_UNIDAD = A.N_UNIDAD, @ID_TIPO_UNIDAD = A.ID_TIPO_UNIDAD, @ID_SERVICIO = A.ID_SERVICIO, @N_SERVICIO = A.N_SERVICIO, @ID_TIPO_SERVICIO = A.ID_TIPO_SERVICIO, @ID_PUESTO = A.ID_PUESTO, @ID_TIPO_PUESTO = A.ID_TIPO_PUESTO, @N_TIPO_PUESTO = A.N_TIPO_PUESTO, @ID_CENT_TRAB_FIS = A.ID_CENT_TRAB_FIS, @ID_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.ID_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @N_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.N_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @DIR_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.DIR_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @ID_RESPONSABLE = A.ID_RESPONSABLE, @ID_LEGAL = A.ID_LEGAL, @NUM_AFILIACION_SS = A.NUM_AFILIACION_SS, @ID_ESTADO_CIVIL = A.ID_ESTADO_CIVIL, @FEC_NACIMIENTO = A.FEC_NACIMIENTO, @CLAVE_SELF = A.CLAVE_SELF, @APELLIDO_1V = A.APELLIDO_1V, @APELLIDO_2V = A.APELLIDO_2V, @NOMBREV = A.NOMBREV, @N_PUESTOV = A.N_PUESTOV, @N_UNIDAD_RAIZV = A.N_UNIDAD_RAIZV, @N_DIRECCIONV = A.N_DIRECCIONV, @N_AREAV = A.N_AREAV, @N_UNIDADV = A.N_UNIDADV, @N_SERVICIOV = A.N_SERVICIOV, @STD_ID_GENDER = B.STD_ID_GENDER, @N_CENTRO_TRABAJOV = A.N_CENTRO_TRABAJOV, @FEC_ANTIGUEDAD = A.FEC_ANTIGUEDAD, @COMPUTA = A.COMPUTA FROM &ORO_EMPLEADOS A, STD_PERSON B WHERE A.ID_EMPLEADO = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.COMPUTA = '1')
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA

- TI: `CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA` · objeto de lectura: `ORO_EMPLEADOS` → `M4ORO_EMPLEADOS`
- Parámetro 1: `P_EMPLEADO`

```sql
SELECT @ID_EMPLEADO = A.ID_EMPLEADO, @ID_ESTADO_CIVIL = A.ID_ESTADO_CIVIL, @STD_N_MARITAL_STAT = C.STD_N_MARITAL_STAT, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @ID_PUESTO = A.ID_PUESTO, @STD_SSN = B.STD_SSN, @STD_DT_BIRTH = B.STD_DT_BIRTH, @N_UNIDAD_RAIZ = A.N_UNIDAD_RAIZ, @N_AREA = A.N_AREA, @NOMBRECOMPLETO =  A.NOMBRE + ' ' + A.APELLIDO_1 + ' ' + A.APELLIDO_2, @SCO_BLOB_PHOTO = B.SCO_BLOB_PHOTO, @N_PUESTO = A.N_PUESTO, @FEC_ANTIGUEDAD = A.FEC_ANTIGUEDAD, @N_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.N_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @DIR_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.DIR_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @CORREO = A.CORREO, @N_DIRECCION = A.N_DIRECCION, @ID_RESPONSABLE = A.ID_RESPONSABLE, @NUM_AFILIACION_SS = A.NUM_AFILIACION_SS, @STD_ID_GENDER = B.STD_ID_GENDER FROM &ORO_EMPLEADOS A, STD_PERSON B, STD_LU_MAR_STAT C WHERE A.ID_EMPLEADO = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND ((   A.ID_EMPLEADO = @P_EMPLEADO   And   A.COMPUTA = '1'   And   (  	 A.ID_ESTADO_CIVIL > '9'  	 And  	 A.ID_ESTADO_CIVIL = C.STD_ID_MARITAL_STAT  	 Or  	  #IFNULL_STRING ( A.ID_ESTADO_CIVIL , '1' ) <= '9'  	 And  	  #CONCAT ( '0' ,  #IFNULL_STRING ( A.ID_ESTADO_CIVIL , '1' ) ) = C.STD_ID_MARITAL_STAT   )  ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_FICHA_WU_RESP

- TI: `CSP_FICHA_WU_RESP` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_HR_RESP` → `M4SCO_H_HR_RESP`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR` = `CSP_FICHA.ID_RESPONSABLE`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR FROM &SCO_H_HR_RESP A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_TYPE_RESP = 'R_FUN' And A.SCO_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_FOTO_ORO

- TI: `CSP_FOTO_ORO` · objeto de lectura: `STD_PERSON` → `STD_PERSON`
- Parámetro 1: `P_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_BLOB_PHOTO = A.SCO_BLOB_PHOTO FROM &STD_PERSON A WHERE (A.STD_ID_PERSON = @P_ID_HR)
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_GRUPO_NIVEL

- TI: `CSP_GRUPO_NIVEL` · objeto de lectura: `SSP_H_CATEGORIA` → `M4SSP_H_CATEGORIA`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR` = `CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA.ID_EMPLEADO`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SSP_NM_CATEGORIA = B.SSP_NM_CATEGORIA, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION FROM &SSP_H_CATEGORIA A, SSP_CATEGORIAS B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SSP_ID_CATEGORIA = B.SSP_ID_CATEGORIA(+) AND ((   A.SSP_FEC_FIN = #DATE('4000-01-01')  ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_H_RESP_UNID

- TI: `CSP_H_RESP_UNID` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_HR_RESP` → `M4SCO_H_HR_RESP`
- Conector: `STD_ID_WORK_UNIT` = `CSP_RESP_UNIDADES.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_CHILD`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR FROM &SCO_H_HR_RESP A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_TYPE_RESP = 'R_FUN' And A.SCO_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_MAIL_PERSONAL

- TI: `CSP_MAIL_PERSONAL` · objeto de lectura: `STD_EMAIL` → `STD_EMAIL`
- Conector: `STD_ID_PERSON` = `CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA.ID_EMPLEADO`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_EMAIL = A.STD_EMAIL FROM &STD_EMAIL A WHERE (( A.STD_DT_END = #DATE ( '4000-01-01' ) And A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = '1'))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_MAIL_RESP

- TI: `CSP_MAIL_RESP` · objeto de lectura: `STD_EMAIL` → `STD_EMAIL`
- Conector: `STD_ID_PERSON` = `CSP_FICHA.ID_RESPONSABLE`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_EMAIL = A.STD_EMAIL FROM &STD_EMAIL A WHERE (A.STD_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_MAIL_RESPONSABLE

- TI: `CSP_MAIL_RESPONSABLE` · objeto de lectura: `STD_EMAIL` → `STD_EMAIL`
- Conector: `STD_ID_PERSON` = `CSP_FICHA_DETALLADA.ID_RESPONSABLE`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_EMAIL = A.STD_EMAIL FROM &STD_EMAIL A WHERE (A.STD_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) And A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = '2')
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_ORO

- TI: `CSP_ORO` · objeto de lectura: `ORO_EMPLEADOS` → `M4ORO_EMPLEADOS`

```sql
SELECT @ID_EMPLEADO = A.ID_EMPLEADO, @N_CENT_TRAB_FIS = A.N_CENT_TRAB_FIS, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @SCO_GB_NAME = B.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_BLOB_PHOTO = B.SCO_BLOB_PHOTO, @NOMBRE_COMPLETO = (A.APELLIDO_2 + ' ' + A.APELLIDO_1 + '  ' + A.NOMBRE), @ID_UNIDAD_RAIZ = A.ID_UNIDAD_RAIZ, @N_PUESTO = A.N_PUESTO, @FEC_ALTA_EMPLEADO = A.FEC_ALTA_EMPLEADO, @APELLIDO_1 = A.APELLIDO_1, @APELLIDO_2 = A.APELLIDO_2, @NOMBRE = A.NOMBRE, @CORREO = A.CORREO, @N_UNIDAD_RAIZ = A.N_UNIDAD_RAIZ, @ID_DIRECCION = A.ID_DIRECCION, @N_DIRECCION = A.N_DIRECCION, @ID_TIPO_DIRECCION = A.ID_TIPO_DIRECCION, @ID_AREA = A.ID_AREA, @N_AREA = A.N_AREA, @ID_TIPO_AREA = A.ID_TIPO_AREA, @ID_UNIDAD = A.ID_UNIDAD, @N_UNIDAD = A.N_UNIDAD, @ID_TIPO_UNIDAD = A.ID_TIPO_UNIDAD, @ID_SERVICIO = A.ID_SERVICIO, @N_SERVICIO = A.N_SERVICIO, @ID_TIPO_SERVICIO = A.ID_TIPO_SERVICIO, @ID_PUESTO = A.ID_PUESTO, @ID_TIPO_PUESTO = A.ID_TIPO_PUESTO, @N_TIPO_PUESTO = A.N_TIPO_PUESTO, @ID_CENT_TRAB_FIS = A.ID_CENT_TRAB_FIS, @ID_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.ID_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @N_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.N_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @DIR_CENTRO_TRABAJO = A.DIR_CENTRO_TRABAJO, @ID_RESPONSABLE = A.ID_RESPONSABLE, @ID_LEGAL = A.ID_LEGAL, @NUM_AFILIACION_SS = A.NUM_AFILIACION_SS, @ID_ESTADO_CIVIL = A.ID_ESTADO_CIVIL, @FEC_NACIMIENTO = A.FEC_NACIMIENTO, @CLAVE_SELF = A.CLAVE_SELF, @APELLIDO_1V = A.APELLIDO_1V, @APELLIDO_2V = A.APELLIDO_2V, @NOMBREV = A.NOMBREV, @N_PUESTOV = A.N_PUESTOV, @N_UNIDAD_RAIZV = A.N_UNIDAD_RAIZV, @N_DIRECCIONV = A.N_DIRECCIONV, @N_AREAV = A.N_AREAV, @N_UNIDADV = A.N_UNIDADV, @N_SERVICIOV = A.N_SERVICIOV, @N_CENTRO_TRABAJOV = A.N_CENTRO_TRABAJOV, @FEC_ANTIGUEDAD = A.FEC_ANTIGUEDAD, @COMPUTA = A.COMPUTA FROM &ORO_EMPLEADOS A, STD_PERSON B WHERE A.ID_EMPLEADO = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.COMPUTA = '1') ORDER BY A.APELLIDO_1 ASC, A.APELLIDO_2 ASC, A.NOMBRE ASC
```

```sql
SELECT @NOMBRECOMPLETO = (#CONCAT_GENERIC (A.SSP_PRIMER_APELLIDO , ' ' , A.STD_N_MAIDEN_NAME , '  ,' , A.STD_N_FIRST_NAME )), @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON FROM &STD_PERSON A
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_ROLES_USUARIO_LOGADO

- TI: `CSP_ROLES_USUARIO_LOGADO` · objeto de lectura: `M4RSC_APP_USR_ROLE` → `M4RSC_APP_USR_ROLE`
- Parámetro 1: `P_APPUSER`

```sql
SELECT @ID_APP_USR = A.ID_APP_USR, @ID_APP_ROLE = A.ID_APP_ROLE, @ID_ORGANIZATION_US = A.ID_ORGANIZATION_US, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @DT_START = A.DT_START FROM &M4RSC_APP_USR_ROLE A WHERE (A.ID_APP_USR = @P_APPUSER And ( A.ID_APP_ROLE = 'M4_USER' Or A.ID_APP_ROLE = 'M4ADM' ) And A.DT_END = #TIME_STAMP ('4000-01-01 0:0:0' ))
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_TELEFONO_EMPRESA

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_PHONE = A.STD_PHONE FROM &STD_PHONE_FAX A WHERE (( A.STD_DT_END = #DATE ( '4000-01-01' ) And A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = '2'))
```


## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_UNID_AREA

- TI: `CSP_UNID_AREA` · objeto de lectura: `STD_REL_WORK_UNIT` → `STD_REL_WORK_UNIT`
- Parámetro 1: `P_DIRECCION`
- Parámetro 2: `P_DIRECCION`
- Parámetro 3: `P_DIRECCION`
- Parámetro 4: `IDENTIFICADOR_AREA`
- Parámetro 5: `CSP_P_SOCIEDAD`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_CHILD = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_CHILD, @POSICION = B.POSICION, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = B.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = B.STD_N_WORK_UNIT FROM &STD_REL_WORK_UNIT A, STD_WORK_UNIT B, STD_LU_WU_TYPE D WHERE A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_CHILD = B.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = D.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND (( A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = @P_DIRECCION Or @P_DIRECCION IS NULL Or @P_DIRECCION = '00' ) And D.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE = @IDENTIFICADOR_AREA And A.ID_ORGANIZATION = @CSP_P_SOCIEDAD And A.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY ()) ORDER BY B.POSICION ASC, A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_CHILD ASC
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!CSP_UNID_DIRE

- TI: `CSP_UNID_DIRE` · objeto de lectura: `STD_WORK_UNIT` → `STD_WORK_UNIT`
- Parámetro 1: `CSP_P_SOCIEDAD`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @POSICION = A.POSICION, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_N_WORK_UNIT FROM &STD_WORK_UNIT A, STD_LU_WU_TYPE B WHERE A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = B.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND (B.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE = 'D' And A.ID_ORGANIZATION = @CSP_P_SOCIEDAD And A.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY ()) ORDER BY A.POSICION ASC, A.STD_N_WORK_UNIT ASC
```

## CSP_QUIEN_ES_QUIEN!SALTA_ORO_EST_CIVIL

- TI: `SALTA_ORO_EST_CIVIL` · objeto de lectura: `STD_HT_MAR_STAT` → `STD_HT_MAR_STAT`
- Parámetro 1: `P_EMPLEADO`
- Parámetro 2: `P_DT_START`
- Parámetro 3: `P_DT_END`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = A.STD_DT_END, @STD_ID_MARITAL_STAT = A.STD_ID_MARITAL_STAT, @STD_N_MARITAL_STAT = B.STD_N_MARITAL_STAT, @DT_LAST_UPDATE = A.DT_LAST_UPDATE FROM &STD_HT_MAR_STAT A, STD_LU_MAR_STAT B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_MARITAL_STAT = B.STD_ID_MARITAL_STAT(+) AND ((   A.STD_ID_HR = @P_EMPLEADO   And   A.STD_DT_START <= @P_DT_START   And   A.STD_DT_END >= @P_DT_END  )) ORDER BY A.STD_DT_START DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @DT_LAST_UPDATE = A.DT_LAST_UPDATE, @DT_LAST_UPDATE_4 = B.DT_LAST_UPDATE, @DT_LAST_UPDATE_1 = C.DT_LAST_UPDATE, @DT_LAST_UPDATE_2 = D.DT_LAST_UPDATE, @DT_LAST_UPDATE_3 = E.DT_LAST_UPDATE FROM &SCO_JOB_PREV_JOBS A, STD_JOB_ACAD_BACKGROUND E, STD_JOB_LANG D, SCO_JOB_CERTIFICATION_LICENSE C, STD_JOB B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE = B.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = B.ID_ORGANIZATION AND C.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) = B.STD_ID_JOB_CODE AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = B.ID_ORGANIZATION AND D.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) = B.STD_ID_JOB_CODE AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = B.ID_ORGANIZATION AND E.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) = B.STD_ID_JOB_CODE AND (A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE = @P_ID_JOB_CODE)
```

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @POSICION = A.POSICION, @STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE = B.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT_1 = D.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE_1 = E.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = C.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT FROM &STD_WORK_UNIT A, STD_LU_WU_TYPE E, STD_WORK_UNIT D, STD_REL_WORK_UNIT C, STD_LU_WU_TYPE B WHERE C.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_CHILD(+) = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND D.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = E.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = D.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = B.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) ORDER BY A.POSICION ASC, A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT ASC
```

- TI: `CSP_CARGA_SERVICIOS` · objeto de lectura: `STD_WORK_UNIT` → `STD_WORK_UNIT`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @POSICION = A.POSICION, @STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE = B.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = C.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT_1 = D.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE_1 = E.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT_1 = F.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT_2 = G.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE_2 = H.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT_2 = I.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT_3 = J.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE_3 = K.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE FROM &STD_WORK_UNIT A, STD_LU_WU_TYPE K, STD_WORK_UNIT J, STD_REL_WORK_UNIT I, STD_LU_WU_TYPE H, STD_WORK_UNIT G, STD_REL_WORK_UNIT F, STD_LU_WU_TYPE E, STD_WORK_UNIT D, STD_REL_WORK_UNIT C, STD_LU_WU_TYPE B WHERE C.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_CHILD(+) = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND J.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = K.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND J.ID_ORGANIZATION = K.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND I.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = J.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND I.ID_ORGANIZATION = J.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND G.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = H.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND G.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = G.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND I.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT(+) = G.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT AND I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = G.ID_ORGANIZATION AND D.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = E.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT = D.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_PARENT(+) = D.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = D.ID_ORGANIZATION AND A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT_TYPE = B.STD_ID_WU_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND (J.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And I.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And G.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And F.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And D.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And C.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And A.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And B.STD_ID_WU_INT_TYPE = 'S') ORDER BY A.POSICION ASC, A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT ASC
```

## CSP_RP_PROYECCIONES!SSE_EXTRAC_PROYECCION

- TI: `SSE_EXTRAC_PROYECCION` · objeto de lectura: `CSP_EXTRAC_PROYECCION` → `M4CSP_EXTRAC_PROYECCION`
- Parámetro 1: `P_ID_HR`
- Parámetro 2: `POR_HR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 3: `PID_GRUP`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @CSP_ORDEN = A.CSP_ORDEN, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @ID_ITEM = A.ID_ITEM, @CSP_ID_GRUPO = A.CSP_ID_GRUPO, @PAGA01 = A.PAGA01, @CSP_ID_SUBGR = A.CSP_ID_SUBGR, @PAGA02 = A.PAGA02, @PAGA03 = A.PAGA03, @PAGA04 = A.PAGA04, @PAGA05 = A.PAGA05, @PAGA06 = A.PAGA06, @PAGA07 = A.PAGA07, @PAGA08 = A.PAGA08, @PAGA09 = A.PAGA09, @PAGA10 = A.PAGA10, @PAGA11 = A.PAGA11, @PAGA12 = A.PAGA12, @PAGA13 = A.PAGA13, @PAGA14 = A.PAGA14, @PAGA15 = A.PAGA15, @PAGA16 = A.PAGA16, @PAGA17 = A.PAGA17, @PAGA18 = A.PAGA18, @PAGA19 = A.PAGA19, @PAGA20 = A.PAGA20, @TOTAL = A.TOTAL FROM &CSP_EXTRAC_PROYECCION A WHERE (A.STD_ID_HR = @P_ID_HR And A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = @POR_HR_PERIOD And A.CSP_ID_GRUPO = @PID_GRUP And A.CSP_ID_SUBGR = @PID_SUBGR) ORDER BY A.CSP_ORDEN ASC
```

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_N_NACIONALITY_1 = Q.STD_N_NACIONALITY, @STD_N_FIRST_NAME_1 = A.STD_N_FIRST_NAME, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1_1 = A.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @STD_N_MAIDEN_NAME_1 = A.STD_N_MAIDEN_NAME, @STD_ID_GENDER_1 = A.STD_ID_GENDER, @STD_DT_BIRTH = A.STD_DT_BIRTH, @STD_ID_COUNTRY_1 = A.STD_ID_COUNTRY, @STD_ID_GEO_DIV_1 = A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV_1 = A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV, @SCO_PHOTO_1 = A.SCO_PHOTO, @SCO_HOME_PAGE_1 = A.SCO_HOME_PAGE, @STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV_1 = G.STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_GENDER = M.STD_N_GENDER, @SCO_GB_NAME = A.SCO_GB_NAME, @STD_SSN = A.STD_SSN, @SSP_NM_TP_DOC = O.SSP_NM_TP_DOC FROM &STD_PERSON A, STD_SUB_GEO_DIV G, STD_LU_GENDER M, SSP_LU_TP_DOC O, STD_COUNTRY Q WHERE A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = G.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = G.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = G.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = M.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_GENDER = M.STD_ID_GENDER(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = O.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SSP_ID_TP_DOC = O.SSP_ID_TP_DOC(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = Q.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SSP_ID_PAIS_EMISOR = Q.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+)
```

- TI: `SCO_DIRECCION` · objeto de lectura: `STD_ADDRESS` → `STD_ADDRESS`
- Conector: `STD_ID_PERSON` = `SCO_DATOS_PERSONALES.STD_ID_PERSON`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_N_GEO_DIV = F.STD_N_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE, @STD_ADDRESS_LINE_1 = A.STD_ADDRESS_LINE_1, @STD_ADDRESS_LINE_2 = A.STD_ADDRESS_LINE_2, @STD_ADDRESS_LINE_3 = A.STD_ADDRESS_LINE_3, @STD_ADDRESS_LINE_4 = A.STD_ADDRESS_LINE_4, @STD_ID_COUNTRY = A.STD_ID_COUNTRY, @STD_ID_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_GEO_PLACE = B.STD_N_GEO_PLACE, @STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV = E.STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_COUNTRY = C.STD_N_COUNTRY, @SSP_NUM_VIA = A.SSP_NUM_VIA, @SSP_ESCALERA = A.SSP_ESCALERA, @SSP_PISO = A.SSP_PISO, @SSP_BLOQUE = A.SSP_BLOQUE, @SSP_PUERTA = A.SSP_PUERTA, @SSP_ID_SIGLA_DOMIC = A.SSP_ID_SIGLA_DOMIC, @SSP_DISTRIT_POSTAL = A.SSP_DISTRIT_POSTAL, @STD_ID_GEO_PLACE = A.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE, @STD_ZIP_CODE = A.STD_ZIP_CODE FROM &STD_ADDRESS A, STD_GEO_PLACE B, STD_COUNTRY C, STD_SUB_GEO_DIV E, STD_GEO_DIV F WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = B.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = B.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE = B.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = B.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = C.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = E.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = E.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = E.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = F.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = F.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND (A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = '1' Or A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE IS NULL) ORDER BY A.STD_OR_ADDRESS ASC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_NM_TIME_UNIT = C.SCO_NM_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_ORD_ACTION = A.SCO_ORD_ACTION, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_NM_ACTION = A.SCO_NM_ACTION, @SCO_ID_ORIGIN = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN, @SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_DT_START_RAT = A.SCO_DT_START_RAT, @SCO_ACTION_DESC = A.SCO_ACTION_DESC, @SCO_OBJECTIVES = A.SCO_OBJECTIVES, @SCO_ACTION_HOW = A.SCO_ACTION_HOW, @SCO_IS_MANDATORY = A.SCO_IS_MANDATORY, @SCO_ACTION_WHEN = A.SCO_ACTION_WHEN, @SCO_APROX_DURATION = A.SCO_APROX_DURATION, @SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_IS_VISIBLE = A.SCO_IS_VISIBLE, @SCO_IS_FINISHED = A.SCO_IS_FINISHED, @SCO_DT_FINISH = A.SCO_DT_FINISH, @SCO_FINISH_DESC = A.SCO_FINISH_DESC FROM &SCO_CR_ACTION_HR A, SCO_X_TIME_UNIT C, SCO_X_SUG_ACTION B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = C.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE(+) AND (A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY ()) ORDER BY A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD ASC, A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE ASC, A.SCO_NM_ACTION ASC
```

## SCO_EMPLOYEE_DOSSIER!SCO_DS_EVALUATON

- TI: `SCO_DS_EVALUATON` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_EVALUATE` → `M4SCO_H_EVALUATE`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR` = `SCO_ID_PERSONA.STD_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_DT_START_EVAL = A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL, @SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC = #MAX(B.SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC), @SCO_NM_EVAL_PLAN = #MAX(C.SCO_NM_EVAL_PLAN), @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = #MAX(J.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1), @STD_N_FIRST_NAME = #MAX(J.STD_N_FIRST_NAME), @SCO_DT_START_PROC = #MAX(A.SCO_DT_START_PROC), @SCO_NM_LEVEL = #MAX(H.SCO_NM_LEVEL), @SCO_NM_SCALE = #MAX(I.SCO_NM_SCALE) FROM &SCO_H_EVALUATE A, SCO_H_EVAL_PROC B, SCO_PLAN_EVAL C, SCO_EVALUATOR D, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL H, SCO_SCALE I, STD_PERSON J WHERE D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND D.SCO_DT_START_EVAL(+) = A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL AND D.SCO_ID_HR(+) = A.SCO_ID_HR AND D.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_PROC = B.SCO_DT_START_PROC(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = B.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = C.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EMMITED_CAP = H.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL_CAP = H.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION = I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND H.SCO_ID_SCALE = I.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND D.SCO_ID_HR = J.STD_ID_PERSON(+) GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_HR, A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN, @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SCO_ID_SOURCE = A.SCO_ID_SOURCE, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_MEANING = A.SCO_MEANING, @SCO_NM_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_NM_EXTD_KN, @SCO_NM_LEVEL = D.SCO_NM_LEVEL, @SCO_NM_SOURCE = E.SCO_NM_SOURCE, @SCO_NM_SOURCE_TYPE = F.SCO_NM_SOURCE_TYPE FROM &SCO_H_HR_KN_LVL A, SCO_KNOW_MAP B, SCO_KNOW_LEVEL C, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL D, SCO_KN_EV_SOURCE E, SCO_SOURCE_TYPE F WHERE A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_1 = C.SCO_DT_START(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = C.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = C.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND C.SCO_ID_LEVEL = D.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND C.SCO_ID_SCALE = D.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_SOURCE = E.SCO_ID_SOURCE(+) AND E.SCO_ID_SOURCE_TYPE = F.SCO_ID_SOURCE_TYPE(+) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC
```

## SCO_EMPLOYEE_DOSSIER!SCO_DS_MENTOR

- TI: `SCO_DS_MENTOR` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_CR_MENTOR` → `M4SCO_CR_MENTOR`
- Conector: `DT_END` = `SCO_DS_CAREER_PLAN.DT_END`
- Conector: `DT_START_PLAN` = `SCO_DS_CAREER_PLAN.DT_START`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_APP = A.SCO_ID_APP, @SCO_DT_START_APP = A.SCO_DT_START_APP, @SCO_DT_START_1 = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @SCO_ID_APP_STATUS = A.SCO_ID_APP_STATUS, @SCO_NM_APP_STATUS = B.SCO_NM_APP_STATUS, @SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT = C.SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT, @STD_ID_PERSON = F.STD_ID_PERSON, @SCO_ID_APP_TYPE = D.SCO_ID_APP_TYPE, @SCO_NM_APP_TYPE = G.SCO_NM_APP_TYPE, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_ID_SOURCE_1 = J.SCO_ID_SOURCE, @SCO_NM_SOURCE = K.SCO_NM_SOURCE, @SCO_DT_START = C.SCO_DT_START FROM &SCO_APP_RECRUIT_PR A, SCO_X_APP_STATUS B, SCO_RECRUIT_PRO C, SCO_HR_APP D, STD_HR E, STD_PERSON F, SCO_X_APP_TYPE G, SCO_APP_SOURCE J, SCO_X_SOURCE K WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_APP_STATUS = B.SCO_ID_APP_STATUS(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = C.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_APP = D.SCO_DT_START_APP(+) AND A.SCO_ID_APP = D.SCO_ID_APP(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND D.SCO_ID_APP = E.STD_ID_HR(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND E.STD_ID_HR = F.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND D.SCO_ID_APP_TYPE = G.SCO_ID_APP_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = J.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_APP = J.SCO_DT_START_APP(+) AND A.SCO_ID_APP = J.SCO_ID_APP(+) AND A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = J.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR(+) AND J.ID_ORGANIZATION = K.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND J.SCO_ID_SOURCE = K.SCO_ID_SOURCE(+) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START_APP DESC
```

- Parámetro 4: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`
- Parámetro 5: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`
- Parámetro 6: `SCO_GTA_ARG_HR_ORDINAL`
- Parámetro 7: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`
- Parámetro 8: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`
- Parámetro 9: `SCO_GTA_ARG_HR_ORDINAL`
- Parámetro 10: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`
- Parámetro 11: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @SCO_ID_WORK_LOCATION = H.SCO_ID_WORK_LOCATION, @STD_N_WORK_LOCATION = I.STD_N_WORK_LOCATION, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = E.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = F.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_N_ROLE = C.SCO_N_ROLE, @SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = D.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = G.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_GB_NAME = A.SCO_GB_NAME FROM &STD_PERSON A, SCO_HR_ROLE C, SCO_H_HR_WUNIT D, SCO_VW_H_HRR_JOB E, STD_WORK_UNIT G, STD_JOB F, SCO_H_HR_WLOCATION H, STD_WORK_LOCATION I WHERE A.STD_ID_PERSON = C.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.STD_ID_PERSON = D.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.STD_ID_PERSON = E.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND D.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = G.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND E.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = F.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.STD_ID_PERSON = H.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION = I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND H.SCO_ID_WORK_LOCATION = I.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION(+) AND (A.STD_ID_PERSON = @SCO_GTA_ARG_ID_HR And C.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @SCO_GTA_ARG_HR_ORDINAL And D.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @SCO_GTA_ARG_HR_ORDINAL And D.SCO_DT_START <= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE And D.SCO_DT_END >= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE And E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @SCO_GTA_ARG_HR_ORDINAL And E.SCO_DT_START <= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE And E.SCO_DT_END >= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE And H.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @SCO_GTA_ARG_HR_ORDINAL And H.SCO_DT_START <= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE And H.SCO_DT_END >= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE)
```

- Parámetro 3: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`
- Parámetro 4: `SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END FROM &SCO_HR_ROLE A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_HR = @SCO_GTA_ARG_ID_HR And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SCO_GTA_ARG_HR_ORDINAL And A.SCO_DT_START <= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE And A.SCO_DT_END >= @SCO_GTA_ARG_START_DATE And A.SCO_MAIN_ROLE = '1')
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_GTA_MANAGEMENT = A.SCO_GTA_MANAGEMENT, @SCO_MANAGEMENT_BY_DAYS = A.SCO_MANAGEMENT_BY_DAYS, @SCO_CLOCKINOUT = A.SCO_CLOCKINOUT, @SCO_CARD_NUMBER = A.SCO_CARD_NUMBER, @SCO_DECLARATIVE = A.SCO_DECLARATIVE, @SCO_TIMESHEET_MONTH = A.SCO_TIMESHEET_MONTH FROM &SCO_H_GTA_DATA A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_HR = @SCO_GTA_PARAM_EMPLOYEE And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SCO_GTA_PARAM_EMPLOYEE_PERIOD And A.SCO_DT_START <= @SCO_GTA_PARAM_DATE_TO_LOAD And A.SCO_DT_END >= @SCO_GTA_PARAM_DATE_TO_LOAD) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC
```

WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_ALERT_SEVERITY_LEVEL = B.SCO_ID_ALERT_SEVERITY_LEVEL(+) AND A.DT_START = C.DT_START(+) AND A.STD_ID_HR = C.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = C.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND A.STD_ID_HR = D.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_ALERT = E.SCO_ID_PROPERTY(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE = G.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND E.SCO_ID_DISPLAY_CLASIFICATION = H.SCO_ID_DISPLAY_CLASIFICATION(+) AND (C.SCO_ID_STATUS = 2) ORDER BY A.STD_ID_HR ASC, A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD ASC, A.DT_START ASC, A.SCO_ID_ALERT_SEVERITY_LEVEL DESC

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @DT_LAST_UPDATE = A.DT_LAST_UPDATE, @SCO_IN_OR_OUT = A.SCO_IN_OR_OUT, @SCO_CLOCK_ACTIV_TP = A.SCO_CLOCK_ACTIV_TP, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_DATE_AND_HOUR = A.SCO_DT_DATE_AND_HOUR, @SCO_ID_STATUS = A.SCO_ID_STATUS, @SCO_ID_ORIGIN = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN FROM &SCO_HR_CLOCK_IN_OUT_DEC A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_STATUS = 2 And A.SCO_DT_DATE_AND_HOUR >= @SCO_DT_START_TO_LOAD And A.SCO_DT_DATE_AND_HOUR <= @SCO_DT_END_TO_LOAD)
```

- Conector: `SCO_DT_START_P` = `SCO_GTA_VIEW_ALERTS.DT_START`

- Conector: `SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD` = `SCO_GTA_VIEW_ALERTS.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_DT_END_P`
- Parámetro 2: `SCO_DT_START_P`

```sql

```

@SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC = D.SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC, @SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_GB_NAME = C.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_ORD_ACTION = A.SCO_ORD_ACTION, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_NM_ACTION = A.SCO_NM_ACTION, @SCO_ID_ORIGIN = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN, @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START_PROC = A.SCO_DT_START_PROC, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_DT_START_RAT = A.SCO_DT_START_RAT, @SCO_ACTION_DESC = A.SCO_ACTION_DESC, @SCO_OBJECTIVES = A.SCO_OBJECTIVES, @SCO_ACTION_HOW = A.SCO_ACTION_HOW, @SCO_IS_MANDATORY = A.SCO_IS_MANDATORY, @SCO_ACTION_WHEN = A.SCO_ACTION_WHEN, @SCO_APROX_DURATION = A.SCO_APROX_DURATION, @SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_IS_VISIBLE = A.SCO_IS_VISIBLE, @SCO_IS_FINISHED = A.SCO_IS_FINISHED, @SCO_DT_FINISH = SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT F, SCO_KNOW_MAP G, SCO_KNOW_LEVEL H, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL I WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = C.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_PROC = D.SCO_DT_START_PROC(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = D.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = E.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = F.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @PAR_OR_HR_PERIOD And ( A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END )) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START ASC, A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE ASC

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = B.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = C.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_GB_NAME = D.SCO_GB_NAME FROM &SCO_HR_ROLE A, SCO_VW_H_HRR_JOB B, STD_JOB C, STD_PERSON D WHERE B.SCO_ID_HR(+) = A.SCO_ID_HR AND B.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND B.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = C.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = D.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.SCO_MAIN_ROLE = '1' And ( A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END ) And A.SCO_ID_HR = @PAR_ID_HR And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @PAR_OR_HR_PERIOD)
```

- Conector: `STD_ID_PERSON` = `SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA.SMCO_HR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 1: `SMCO_TODAY_DATE`
- Parámetro 2: `SMCO_TODAY_DATE`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_EMAIL = A.STD_EMAIL FROM &STD_EMAIL A WHERE (A.STD_DT_START <= @SMCO_TODAY_DATE And A.STD_DT_END >= @SMCO_TODAY_DATE And ( A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = '2' Or A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE IS NULL )) ORDER BY A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE ASC, A.STD_DT_START DESC
```

- Parámetro 1: `SMCO_TODAY_DATE`
- Parámetro 2: `SMCO_TODAY_DATE`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_ID_COUNTRY = A.STD_ID_COUNTRY, @STD_N_COUNTRY = B.STD_N_COUNTRY, @STD_N_NACIONALITY = B.STD_N_NACIONALITY, @STD_PRIMARY_COUNTRY = A.STD_PRIMARY_COUNTRY FROM &STD_HR_NATIONALITY A, STD_COUNTRY B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = B.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND (A.DT_START <= @SMCO_TODAY_DATE And A.DT_END >= @SMCO_TODAY_DATE) ORDER BY A.STD_PRIMARY_COUNTRY DESC
```

- Parámetro 1: `SMCO_TODAY_DATE`
- Parámetro 2: `SMCO_TODAY_DATE`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_ID_LINE_TYPE = A.STD_ID_LINE_TYPE, @STD_INT_COUNTRY_CODE = A.STD_INT_COUNTRY_CODE, @STD_INT_REGION_CODE = A.STD_INT_REGION_CODE, @STD_NAT_REGION_CODE = A.STD_NAT_REGION_CODE, @STD_PHONE = A.STD_PHONE FROM &STD_PHONE_FAX A WHERE (A.STD_DT_START <= @SMCO_TODAY_DATE And A.STD_DT_END >= @SMCO_TODAY_DATE And ( A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE = '2' Or A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE IS NULL ) And ( A.STD_ID_LINE_TYPE = '001' Or A.STD_ID_LINE_TYPE = '003' )) ORDER BY A.STD_ID_LINE_TYPE ASC, A.STD_DT_START DESC, A.STD_ID_LOCATION_TYPE ASC
```

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @STD_OR_INCIDENT = A.STD_OR_INCIDENT, @STD_ID_ACTION_TYPE = A.STD_ID_ACTION_TYPE, @STD_N_ACTION_TYPE = B.STD_N_ACTION_TYPE, @STD_DT_START_ACTION = A.STD_DT_START_ACTION, @STD_DT_END_ACTION = A.STD_DT_END_ACTION, @STD_ID_ACT_END_TYPE = A.STD_ID_ACT_END_TYPE, @STD_N_ACT_END_TYPE = C.STD_N_ACT_END_TYPE, @SCO_ID_CHG_REAS = A.SCO_ID_CHG_REAS, @SCO_NM_ACT_CHG_REAS = D.SCO_NM_ACT_CHG_REAS, @STD_COMMENT = A.STD_COMMENT FROM &STD_HT_DISC_ACTION A, SCO_X_ACT_CHG_REAS D, STD_LU_ACT_END_TYPE C, STD_LU_ACTION_TYPE B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_CHG_REAS = D.SCO_ID_ACT_CHG_REAS(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_ACT_END_TYPE = C.STD_ID_ACT_END_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_ACTION_TYPE = B.STD_ID_ACTION_TYPE(+) ORDER BY A.STD_DT_START_ACTION DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @STD_OR_INCIDENT = A.STD_OR_INCIDENT, @STD_DT_INCIDENT = A.STD_DT_INCIDENT, @STD_DT_END = A.STD_DT_END, @STD_DT_REPORTED = A.STD_DT_REPORTED, @STD_ID_INCIDENT_TYPE = A.STD_ID_INCIDENT_TYPE, @STD_N_INCIDENT_TYPE = B.STD_N_INCIDENT_TYPE, @STD_ID_HR_IMPLIED = A.STD_ID_HR_IMPLIED, @SCO_GB_NAME = D.SCO_GB_NAME, @STD_COMMENT = A.STD_COMMENT FROM &STD_HR_PERIOD_DISC_INCIDENT A, STD_PERSON D, STD_HR C, STD_LU_INCIDENT_TYPE B WHERE C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_HR = D.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_HR_IMPLIED = C.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_INCIDENT_TYPE = B.STD_ID_INCIDENT_TYPE(+) ORDER BY A.STD_DT_INCIDENT DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = B.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = C.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_N_ROLE = B.SCO_N_ROLE, @SCO_MAIN_ROLE = B.SCO_MAIN_ROLE, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE FROM &SCO_VW_H_HRR_JOB A, SCO_HR_ROLE B, STD_JOB C WHERE A.SCO_ID_HR = B.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = B.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = C.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND (B.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC, B.SCO_MAIN_ROLE DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_NUM_HEADCOUNT = F.SCO_NUM_HEADCOUNT, @SCO_NUM_EJC = F.SCO_NUM_EJC, @SCO_ID_COMPL = F.SCO_ID_COMPL, @SCO_NM_COMPL = G.SCO_NM_COMPL, @SCO_NUM_HOURS = F.SCO_NUM_HOURS, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = E.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_ID_POSITION = A.SCO_ID_POSITION, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_NM_POSITION = B.SCO_NM_POSITION, @STD_N_WORK_LOCATION = C.STD_N_WORK_LOCATION, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = D.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_N_ROLE = E.SCO_N_ROLE, @SCO_MAIN_ROLE = E.SCO_MAIN_ROLE FROM &SCO_H_HR_POSITION A, SCO_POSITION B, STD_WORK_LOCATION C, STD_WORK_UNIT D, SCO_HR_ROLE E, SCO_H_HR_POS_COMP F, SCO_X_COMP_POS_TYPE G WHERE F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND F.SCO_ID_HR(+) = A.SCO_ID_HR AND F.SCO_ID_POSITION(+) = A.SCO_ID_POSITION AND F.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_POSITION = B.SCO_ID_POSITION(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_WORK_LOCATION = C.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = D.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = E.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.SCO_ID_COMPL = G.SCO_ID_COMPL(+) AND (E.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC, E.SCO_MAIN_ROLE DESC
```

## SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA!SMCO_PROFS_INFO_PREFERENCES

- TI: `SMCO_PROFS_INFO_PREFERENCES` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_CR_PREFERENC` → `M4SCO_CR_PREFERENC`
- Conector: `STD_ID_HR` = `SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA.SMCO_HR_PERIOD`
- Conector: `STD_OR_HR_PERIOD` = `SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA.SMCO_OR_HR_PERIOD`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @SCO_PREF_PRIORITY = A.SCO_PREF_PRIORITY, @SCO_CK_NAT_INT = A.SCO_CK_NAT_INT, @STD_N_COUNTRY = B.STD_N_COUNTRY, @STD_N_GEO_DIV = C.STD_N_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV = D.STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @STD_ID_JOB_CODE = A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = E.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = F.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_PREFERENCES = A.SCO_PREFERENCES FROM &SCO_CR_PREFERENC A, STD_COUNTRY B, STD_GEO_DIV C, STD_SUB_GEO_DIV D, STD_JOB E, STD_WORK_UNIT F WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = B.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = C.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = C.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = D.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = D.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = D.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE = E.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = F.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) ORDER BY A.SCO_PREF_PRIORITY ASC
```

## SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA!SMCO_PROFS_INFO_ROLE_WORK_TIME

- TI: `SMCO_PROFS_INFO_ROLE_WORK_TIME` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_HR_ROLE_TIME` → `M4SCO_H_HR_RO_TIME`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR` = `SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA.SMCO_HR_PERIOD`
- Conector: `SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER` = `SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA.SMCO_OR_HR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 1: `SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_N_ROLE = B.SCO_N_ROLE, @SCO_MAIN_ROLE = B.SCO_MAIN_ROLE, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = B.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS = A.SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS, @SCO_FULL_TIME = A.SCO_FULL_TIME, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_NM_REASON_CHANGE = C.SCO_NM_REASON_CHANGE, @SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD = A.SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD FROM &SCO_H_HR_ROLE_TIME A, SCO_HR_ROLE B, SCO_X_REASON_FOR_CHANGE C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = B.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = B.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_REASON_CHANGE = C.SCO_ID_REASON_CHANGE(+) AND (B.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC, B.SCO_MAIN_ROLE DESC
```


## SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA!SMCO_PROFS_INFO_TODAY_MAIN_ROL

- TI: `SMCO_PROFS_INFO_TODAY_MAIN_ROL` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_HR_ROLE` → `M4SCO_HR_ROLE`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR` = `SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA.SMCO_HR_PERIOD`
- Conector: `SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER` = `SMCO_EMPLOYEE_PROFESIONAL_DATA.SMCO_OR_HR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 1: `SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD FROM &SCO_HR_ROLE A WHERE (A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () And A.SCO_MAIN_ROLE = '1' And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SMCO_OR_PERIOD_TO_FILTER)
```


- Parámetro 2: `SMCO_OR_ROLE`
- Parámetro 3: `SMCO_DT_EVAL`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_EVALUATOR = A.SCO_OR_EVALUATOR, @SCO_GB_NAME = B.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC = D.SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_DT_START_EVAL = A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL, @SCO_ID_EVALUATOR = A.SCO_ID_EVALUATOR FROM &SCO_EVALUATOR A, STD_PERSON B, SCO_H_EVAL_PROC D, SCO_H_EVALUATE C WHERE A.SCO_ID_HR = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_DT_START_PROC = D.SCO_DT_START_PROC(+) AND C.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = D.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL = C.SCO_DT_START_EVAL(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = C.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = C.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_EVALUATOR = $ID_PERSON And A.SCO_ID_HR = @SMCO_ID_HR And A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @SMCO_OR_ROLE And A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL = @SMCO_DT_EVAL)
```

## SMCO_GRAPH_EVAL_GAUSS!SMCO_EVAL_DATA

- TI: `SMCO_EVAL_DATA` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_EVALUATOR` → `M4SSE_EVALUATOR`
- Conector: `SMCO_DT_START_PROC` = `SMCO_GRAPH_EVAL_GAUSS.SMCO_DT_START_PROC`
- Conector: `SMCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN` = `SMCO_GRAPH_EVAL_GAUSS.SMCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN`

```sql
SELECT @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = E.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = F.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_NM_LEVEL = C.SCO_NM_LEVEL, @SCO_NM_LEVEL_1 = D.SCO_NM_LEVEL, @SCO_GB_NAME = B.SCO_GB_NAME, @ACCION_ACEPTADO = A.ACCION_ACEPTADO, @NIVEL_ACEPTADO = A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO, @MOTIVO_ACCION = A.MOTIVO_ACCION, @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_ID_EVALUATOR = A.SCO_ID_EVALUATOR, @SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC = A.SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC, @SCO_ID_LEVEL_OBJ = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL_OBJ, @SCO_VALUE_OBJ_QUANT = A.SCO_VALUE_OBJ_QUANT, @SCO_ID_EMMITED_OBJ = A.SCO_ID_EMMITED_OBJ, @SCO_ID_LEVEL_CAP = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL_CAP, @SCO_CALCUL_CAP = A.SCO_CALCUL_CAP, @SCO_ID_EMMITED_CAP = A.SCO_ID_EMMITED_CAP, @SCO_CALCUL_OBJ = A.SCO_CALCUL_OBJ, @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START_PROC = A.SCO_DT_START_PROC FROM &SSE_EVALUATOR A, STD_PERSON B, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL C, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL D, SCO_H_EVALUATE E, STD_JOB F WHERE A.SCO_ID_HR = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EMMITED_CAP = C.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL_CAP = C.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EMMITED_OBJ = D.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL_OBJ = D.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL = E.SCO_DT_START_EVAL(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = E.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND E.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = F.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND (A.SCO_EVALUATION_DEF = '1') ORDER BY B.SCO_GB_NAME ASC, A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO ASC
```

## SMCO_GRAPH_EVAL_GAUSS!SMCO_GR_EV_PORC_DIST

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_ID_SCALE = A.SCO_ID_SCALE, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_AWAITED_PERC = A.SCO_AWAITED_PERC, @SCO_ENTRANCE_SCORE = A.SCO_ENTRANCE_SCORE, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT FROM &SCO_PERC_DIST A WHERE (A.SCO_DT_START <= @SMCO_DT_START_PROC And @SMCO_DT_START_PROC <= A.SCO_DT_END)
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START_PROC = B.SCO_DT_START_PROC, @SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC = B.SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC, @SCO_NM_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_NM_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_ID_SCALE = A.SCO_ID_SCALE FROM &SCO_PLAN_EVAL A, SCO_H_EVAL_PROC B WHERE B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND B.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN(+) = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN AND (A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = @SMCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN And B.SCO_DT_START_PROC = @SMCO_DT_START_PROC)
```

## SMCO_IV_INTERV_RES!M4T_APP_INT_PTE_RES

- TI: `M4T_APP_INT_PTE_RES` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_APP_INTERVIEW` → `M4SCO_APP_INTERVIE`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_APP = A.SCO_ID_APP, @SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER = E.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER, @SCO_DT_START_APP = A.SCO_DT_START_APP, @SCO_OR_INTERVIEW = A.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW, @SCO_ID_INTER_RES = A.SCO_ID_INTER_RES, @SCO_OR_HR_DOC = A.SCO_OR_HR_DOC, @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @SCO_GB_NAME = B.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_NM_INTERVIEW = C.SCO_NM_INTERVIEW, @SCO_DT_APPOINTMENT = C.SCO_DT_APPOINTMENT, @SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT = D.SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT FROM &SCO_APP_INTERVIEW A, STD_PERSON B, SCO_INTERVIEW C, SCO_RECRUIT_PRO D, SCO_INTERVIEWERS E WHERE A.SCO_ID_APP = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW = C.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = C.ID_ORGANIZATION AND E.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW(+) = C.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = D.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR(+) AND (E.SCO_ID_HR_INTERV = $ID_PERSON And Not ( EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCO_INTERVIEWERS J, SCO_APP_INT_RESUL I WHERE (I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND I.SCO_DT_START_APP(+) = A.SCO_DT_START_APP AND I.SCO_ID_APP(+) = A.SCO_ID_APP AND I.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW(+) = A.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW AND I.ID_ORGANIZATION = J.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND I.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW = J.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW(+) AND I.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER = J.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER(+) AND (I.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER = J.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER And J.SCO_ID_HR_INTERV = $ID_PERSON ))))) ORDER BY C.SCO_DT_APPOINTMENT DESC
```

- TI: `M4T_APP_INTERV_RES` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_APP_INTERVIEW` → `M4SCO_APP_INTERVIE`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_APP = A.SCO_ID_APP, @SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER = E.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER, @SCO_DT_APPOINTMENT = C.SCO_DT_APPOINTMENT, @SCO_DT_START_APP = A.SCO_DT_START_APP, @SCO_OR_INTERVIEW = A.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW, @SCO_ID_INTER_RES = A.SCO_ID_INTER_RES, @SCO_OR_HR_DOC = A.SCO_OR_HR_DOC, @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @SCO_GB_NAME = B.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_NM_INTERVIEW = C.SCO_NM_INTERVIEW, @SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT = D.SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT FROM &SCO_APP_INTERVIEW A, STD_PERSON B, SCO_INTERVIEW C, SCO_RECRUIT_PRO D, SCO_INTERVIEWERS E, SCO_APP_INT_RESUL F WHERE F.ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND F.SCO_DT_START_APP = A.SCO_DT_START_APP AND F.SCO_ID_APP = A.SCO_ID_APP AND F.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW = A.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW AND A.SCO_ID_APP = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW = C.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = C.ID_ORGANIZATION AND E.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW(+) = C.SCO_OR_INTERVIEW AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = D.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR(+) AND (E.SCO_ID_HR_INTERV = $ID_PERSON And F.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER = E.SCO_OR_INTERVIEWER) ORDER BY C.SCO_DT_APPOINTMENT DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT_1 = B.SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT, @SCO_DT_START = B.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = B.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_ID_JOB = B.SCO_ID_JOB, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = C.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_ID_APP = A.SCO_ID_APP, @SCO_DT_START_APP = A.SCO_DT_START_APP, @SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT = B.SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT FROM &SCO_APP_RECRUIT_PR A, SCO_RECRUIT_PRO B, STD_JOB C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = B.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_JOB = C.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND (A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () And B.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And B.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () And B.SCO_ID_HR_REQUEST = $ID_PERSON) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_NM_TIME_UNIT = C.SCO_NM_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_ORD_ACTION = A.SCO_ORD_ACTION, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_NM_ACTION = A.SCO_NM_ACTION, @SCO_ID_ORIGIN = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN, @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START_PROC = A.SCO_DT_START_PROC, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_DT_START_RAT = A.SCO_DT_START_RAT, @SCO_ACTION_DESC = A.SCO_ACTION_DESC, @SCO_OBJECTIVES = A.SCO_OBJECTIVES, @SCO_ACTION_HOW = A.SCO_ACTION_HOW, @SCO_IS_MANDATORY = A.SCO_IS_MANDATORY, @SCO_ACTION_WHEN = A.SCO_ACTION_WHEN, @SCO_APROX_DURATION = A.SCO_APROX_DURATION, @SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_IS_VISIBLE = A.SCO_IS_VISIBLE, @SCO_IS_FINISHED = A.SCO_IS_FINISHED, @SCO_DT_FINISH = A.SCO_DT_FINISH, @SCO_FINISH_DESC = A.SCO_FINISH_DESC, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT FROM &SCO_CR_ACTION_HR A, SCO_X_TIME_UNIT C, SCO_X_SUG_ACTION B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = C.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_HR = @FILTRO_SCO_ID_HR And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @FILTRO_SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD_N And ( A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () )) ORDER BY A.SCO_IS_FINISHED ASC
```

## SRCO_PA_MODIFICATION!SRCO_PA_PM_BPO_DETAILS

- TI: `SRCO_PA_PM_BPO_DETAILS` · objeto de lectura: `SWF_BPO` → `M4RWF_BPO`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @ID_BPC = A.ID_BPC, @ID_EVENT = G.ID_EVENT, @ICON_KEY = B.ICON_KEY, @ID_TASK = E.ID_TASK, @ID_WORKITEM = D.ID_WORKITEM, @ID_TYPE = B.ID_TYPE, @DT_LAST_ACTION = (#IFNULL_DATE (A.DT_CANCELLATION , C.DT_INSTANTIATION )), @N_STATE = (#IFNULL_STRING (C.N_STATE , B.N_STATE )), @STATE_DESC = D.STATE_DESC, @ID_BPO = A.ID_BPO, @ID_BPO_STATUS = A.ID_BPO_STATUS FROM &SWF_BPO A, SWF_STATE B, SWF_INST_TASK C, SWF_WORKITEM D, SWF_TASK E, SWF_TRANSITION F, SWF_EVENTASSOC G WHERE A.ID_BPC = B.ID_BPC(+) AND A.ID_ACTIVATE_STATE = B.ID_STATE(+) AND E.ID_BPC(+) = B.ID_BPC AND E.ID_STATE(+) = B.ID_STATE AND A.ID_BPC = C.ID_BPC(+) AND A.ID_BPO = C.ID_BPO(+) AND A.ID_ACTIVATE_STATE = C.ID_STATE(+) AND C.ID_BPC = D.ID_BPC(+) AND C.ID_BPO = D.ID_BPO(+) AND C.ID_INST_TASK = D.ID_INST_TASK(+) AND A.ID_BPC = F.ID_BPC(+) AND A.ID_ACTIVATE_STATE = F.ID_FROM(+) AND G.ID_BPC(+) = F.ID_BPC AND G.ID_TRANSITION(+) = F.ID_TRANSITION AND (D.ID_USER (+) = $ID_APP_USER And D.ID_WKITEM_STATUS (+) = 1)
```


- Conector: `ID_NODE` = `SRCO_PA_PM_STEPS_HIRE.ID_NODE`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_HR_TYPE` = `SRCO_PA_PM_STEPS_HIRE.SRCO_P_ID_HR_TYPE`
- Conector: `SCO_ID_T3` = `SRCO_PA_PM_STEPS_HIRE.SRCO_P_ID_HIRE`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_T3 = A.SCO_ID_T3, @SCO_ORDER = A.SCO_ORDER, @SCO_ID_HR_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_HR_TYPE, @ID_NODE = D.ID_NODE FROM &SCO_HR_TP_HIRE_S A, SCO_HIRE_STEPS B, SCH_T3_INHERIT C, SCH_NODES D, SCH_CONNECTORS E WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION AND A.SCO_ID_STEP = B.SCO_ID_STEP AND A.SCO_ID_T3 = B.SCO_ID_T3 AND B.SCO_ID_T3 = C.ID_T3_BASE AND E.ID_NODE = D.ID_NODE AND E.ID_T3 = D.ID_T3 AND E.ID_TI = D.ID_TI AND (C.ID_LEVEL = (SELECT #MIN(CC.ID_LEVEL) FROM SCH_T3_INHERIT CC,SCO_HIRE_STEPS BB WHERE C.ID_T3 = CC.ID_T3 AND CC.ID_T3_BASE = BB.SCO_ID_T3) And ( ( B.ID_NODE = E.ID_NODE And E.ID_NODE <> E.ID_NODE_USED ) Or ( B.ID_NODE = E.ID_NODE_USED And E.ID_CONNECTION_TYPE <> 3 ) ))
```

@STD_N_JOB_CODE = C.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC = D.SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC, @SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_NM_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE, @SCO_ORD_ACTION = A.SCO_ORD_ACTION, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_NM_ACTION = A.SCO_NM_ACTION, @SCO_ID_ORIGIN = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN, @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START_PROC = A.SCO_DT_START_PROC, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_DT_START_RAT = A.SCO_DT_START_RAT, @SCO_ACTION_DESC = A.SCO_ACTION_DESC, @SCO_OBJECTIVES = A.SCO_OBJECTIVES, @SCO_ACTION_HOW = A.SCO_ACTION_HOW, @SCO_IS_MANDATORY = A.SCO_IS_MANDATORY, @SCO_ACTION_WHEN = A.SCO_ACTION_WHEN, @SCO_APROX_DURATION = A.SCO_APROX_DURATION, @SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_IS_FINISHED = A.SCO_IS_FINISHED, @SCO_IS_VISIBLE = A.SCO_IS_VISIBLE, @SCO_DT_FINISH = A.SCO_DT_FINISH, @SCO_FINISH_DESC = A.SCO_FINISH_DESC, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT FROM &SCO_CR_ACTION_HR A, SCO_X_SUG_ACTION B, STD_JOB C, SCO_H_EVAL_PROC D, SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT E, SCO_KNOW_MAP F, SCO_KNOW_LEVEL G, SCO_X_TIME_UNIT H WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE = B.SCO_ID_ACTION_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = C.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_PROC = D.SCO_DT_START_PROC(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = D.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = E.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = F.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_RAT = G.SCO_DT_START(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = G.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = G.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = H.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT(+) AND (A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And ( #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END ) And A.SCO_IS_VISIBLE = '0' And A.SCO_ID_HR = $ID_PERSON) ORDER BY A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN ASC, A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE ASC, A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN ASC, A.SCO_DT_START_PROC ASC

## SSCO_H_EVALUTE!SMCO_EVALUATE_TEMP_FILTER

- TI: `SMCO_EVALUATE_TEMP_FILTER` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_EVALUATOR` → `M4SSE_EVALUATOR`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_GB_NAME = B.SCO_GB_NAME FROM &SSE_EVALUATOR A, STD_PERSON B WHERE A.SCO_ID_HR = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.ID_ESTADO_REG = 'PENDIENTE' And ( ( A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO = 0 And A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = $ID_PERSON ) Or ( A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO = 1 And A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = $ID_PERSON ) ) And A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = 'INSERTAR') GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_HR, B.SCO_GB_NAME ORDER BY B.SCO_GB_NAME ASC
```

## SSCO_H_EVALUTE!SMCO_EVALUATOR_TEMP_FILTER

- TI: `SMCO_EVALUATOR_TEMP_FILTER` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_EVALUATOR` → `M4SSE_EVALUATOR`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO FROM &SSE_EVALUATOR A WHERE (A.ID_ESTADO_REG = 'PENDIENTE' And ( ( A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = $ID_PERSON And A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO = 0 ) Or ( A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO = 1 And A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = $ID_PERSON ) ) And A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = 'INSERTAR') GROUP BY A.STD_ID_PERSON, A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO ORDER BY A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO ASC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_COMMENT_2 = A.SCO_COMMENT, @SCO_COMMENT = B.SCO_COMMENT, @SCO_COMMENT_1 = C.SCO_COMMENT, @SCO_DESCRIPTION = B.SCO_DESCRIPTION, @SCO_ID_TYPE = B.SCO_ID_TYPE, @SCO_NM_TYPE = D.SCO_NM_TYPE, @SCO_VALUE = A.SCO_VALUE, @SCO_NM_OBJECTIVE = B.SCO_NM_OBJECTIVE, @SCO_NM_MAGNITUDE = C.SCO_NM_MAGNITUDE, @SCO_ID_CRITERIA_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_CRITERIA_TYPE, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_DT_START_EVAL = A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL, @SCO_ID_EVALUATOR = A.SCO_ID_EVALUATOR, @SCO_OR_EVALUATOR = A.SCO_OR_EVALUATOR, @SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE = A.SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE, @SCO_ID_MAGNITUD = A.SCO_ID_MAGNITUD, @SCO_ACCOMP_DEGREE = A.SCO_ACCOMP_DEGREE, @SCO_SCHED_VALUE = A.SCO_SCHED_VALUE, @SCO_WEIGHT = A.SCO_WEIGHT, @SCO_EXPLANATION = A.SCO_EXPLANATION FROM &SCO_EVAL_OBJECT A, SCO_OBJECTIVE B, SCO_X_OBJ_MAGN C, SCO_X_OBJEC_TYPE D WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE = B.SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_MAGNITUD = C.SCO_ID_MAGNITUD(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_TYPE = D.SCO_ID_TYPE(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_MAGNITUD IS NOT NULL) ORDER BY D.SCO_NM_TYPE ASC, B.SCO_NM_OBJECTIVE ASC
```

- TI: `CSP_CARGA_CP` · objeto de lectura: `STD_GEO_PLACE` → `STD_GEO_PLACE`
- Parámetro 1: `P_COD_POS`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_COUNTRY = A.STD_ID_COUNTRY, @STD_ID_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_GEO_PLACE = A.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE, @STD_N_GEO_PLACE = A.STD_N_GEO_PLACE, @STD_ID_LEGAL_PLACE = A.STD_ID_LEGAL_PLACE, @STD_N_COUNTRY = B.STD_N_COUNTRY, @STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV = C.STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_GEO_DIV = D.STD_N_GEO_DIV FROM &STD_GEO_PLACE A, STD_COUNTRY B, STD_GEO_DIV D, STD_SUB_GEO_DIV C WHERE A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = B.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND C.STD_ID_COUNTRY = D.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND C.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = D.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = C.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = C.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = C.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND (A.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE = @P_COD_POS) ORDER BY A.STD_ID_COUNTRY ASC, A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV ASC, A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV ASC, A.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE ASC
```
