```sql
SELECT @SUS_ID_HR = A.SUS_ID_HR, @SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD = A.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD, @SUS_N_PLAN = F.SUS_N_PLAN, @SUS_N_OPTION = E.SUS_N_OPTION, @SUS_N_COV_CAT = H.SUS_N_COV_CAT, @SUS_ID_OPTION = A.SUS_ID_OPTION, @SUS_ID_COV_CAT = A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT, @SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SUS_ID_PLAN = A.SUS_ID_PLAN, @SUS_ID_PERSON_COV = A.SUS_ID_PERSON_COV, @SUS_DT_START_PLAN = A.SUS_DT_START_PLAN, @SUS_DT_START = A.SUS_DT_START, @SUS_DT_END = A.SUS_DT_END, @SUS_DT_END_PLAN = A.SUS_DT_END_PLAN, @SCO_GB_NAME = B.SCO_GB_NAME, @SUS_COVERAGE = A.SUS_COVERAGE, @SUS_ID_CURRENCY = A.SUS_ID_CURRENCY, @SUS_CUR_DATE = A.SUS_CUR_DATE, @SUS_EX_TYPE = A.SUS_EX_TYPE, @STD_DT_START = I.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = I.STD_DT_END, @SCO_AMT_DEP_LEG = A.SCO_AMT_DEP_LEG, @SCO_AMT_DEP_EMP = A.SCO_AMT_DEP_EMP, @STD_ID_COMP_D_LEG = A.STD_ID_COMP_D_LEG, @STD_ID_COMP_D_EMP = A.STD_ID_COMP_D_EMP, @SCO_CK_DISCOUNTED = A.SCO_CK_DISCOUNTED FROM &SUS_DEP_BENE_COV A, STD_PERSON B, SUS_PLAN_OPTION E, SUS_PLAN F, SUS_PLAN_OPT_COV G, SUS_COV_CAT H, STD_HR_PERIOD I WHERE A.SUS_ID_PERSON_COV = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.SUS_ID_OPTION = E.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN = E.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN = F.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = G.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND A.SUS_ID_OPTION = G.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN = G.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND G.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND G.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = H.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND A.SUS_ID_HR = I.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD = I.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND (A.SUS_DT_START >= @SSE_P_DT_START And A.SUS_DT_END <= @SSE_P_DT_END) ORDER BY A.SUS_ID_PLAN ASC, A.SUS_DT_START_PLAN ASC, A.SUS_ID_PERSON_COV ASC
```

```sql
SELECT @ORDINAL = A.ORDINAL, @MAIL_EMPLEADO = A.MAIL_EMPLEADO, @SSE_ID_PLAN_PERIOD = A.SSE_ID_PLAN_PERIOD, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE2 = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE2, @ACCION_ACEPTADO = A.ACCION_ACEPTADO, @MOTIVO_ACCION = A.MOTIVO_ACCION, @NIVEL_ACEPTADO = A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO, @PERSIST_BATCH = A.PERSIST_BATCH, @MAIL_BATCH = A.MAIL_BATCH, @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG, @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @FEC_AUDIT_NOTIF = A.FEC_AUDIT_NOTIF, @SSE_ID_HR = A.SSE_ID_HR, @SSE_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SSE_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SSE_ID_PLAN = A.SSE_ID_PLAN, @SSE_ID_PERSON_COV = A.SSE_ID_PERSON_COV, @SSE_DT_START_PLAN = A.SSE_DT_START_PLAN, @SSE_DT_START = A.SSE_DT_START, @SSE_DT_END = A.SSE_DT_END, @SSE_DT_END_PLAN = A.SSE_DT_END_PLAN, @SSE_ID_OPTION = A.SSE_ID_OPTION, @SSE_ID_COV_CAT = A.SSE_ID_COV_CAT, @SSE_COV_ELSEW = A.SSE_COV_ELSEW, @SSE_PCT_COV = A.SSE_PCT_COV, @SSE_CONTINGENT = A.SSE_CONTINGENT, @SSE_COMMENT = A.SSE_COMMENT, @SUS_N_PLAN = B.SUS_N_PLAN, @SUS_N_OPTION = F.SUS_N_OPTION, @SUS_N_COV_CAT = H.SUS_N_COV_CAT, @SCO_GB_NAME = C.SCO_GB_NAME, @N_ACCION = D.N_ACCION, @SSE_COVERAGE = A.SSE_COVERAGE, @SSE_ID_CURRENCY = A.SSE_ID_CURRENCY, @SSE_CUR_DATE = A.SSE_CUR_DATE, @SSE_EX_TYPE = A.SSE_EX_TYPE, @SSE_AMT_DEP_LEG = A.SSE_AMT_DEP_LEG, @SSE_AMT_DEP_EMP = A.SSE_AMT_DEP_EMP, @SSE_ID_COMP_D_LEG = A.SSE_ID_COMP_D_LEG, @SSE_ID_COMP_D_EMP = A.SSE_ID_COMP_D_EMP FROM &SSE_DEP_BENE_COV A, SUS_PLAN B, STD_PERSON C, SSE_ACCIONES D, SUS_PLAN_OPTION F, SUS_PLAN_OPT_COV G, SUS_COV_CAT H WHERE A.SSE_ID_PLAN = B.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND A.SSE_ID_PERSON_COV = C.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = D.ACCION_ACEPTADO(+) AND A.SSE_ID_OPTION = F.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) AND A.SSE_ID_PLAN = F.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND A.SSE_ID_COV_CAT = G.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND A.SSE_ID_OPTION = G.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) AND A.SSE_ID_PLAN = G.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND G.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND G.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = H.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND (A.SSE_DT_START >= @SSE_P_DT_START_PLAN And A.SSE_DT_END <= @SSE_P_DT_END_PLAN) ORDER BY A.SSE_ID_PLAN ASC, A.SSE_DT_START_PLAN ASC, A.SSE_ID_PERSON_COV ASC
```

## SSE_BFT_DEP_BENE_COV!SSE_DEP_BENE_COV_MAX

- TI: `SSE_DEP_BENE_COV_MAX` · objeto de lectura: `SUS_COV_DEP` → `M4SUS_COV_DEP`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @SUS_ID_COV_CAT = A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT, @STD_ID_FAMILY_PERSON = E.STD_ID_FAMILY_PERSON, @STD_N_ACT_DEP_TYPE = C.STD_N_ACT_DEP_TYPE, @SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE = A.SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE, @SUS_DT_START = A.SUS_DT_START, @SUS_DT_END = A.SUS_DT_END, @SUS_NUM = A.SUS_NUM, @SUS_MAX_COV = B.SUS_MAX_COV, @SUS_N_COV_CAT = B.SUS_N_COV_CAT FROM &SUS_COV_DEP A, SUS_COV_CAT B, STD_LU_ACT_DEP_TYPE C, STD_LU_DEP_TYPE D, STD_FAMILY E WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = B.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE = C.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = C.ID_ORGANIZATION AND D.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE(+) = C.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = D.ID_ORGANIZATION AND E.STD_ID_DEP_TYPE(+) = D.STD_ID_DEP_TYPE ORDER BY A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT ASC, A.SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE ASC
```

## SSE_BFT_DEP_BENE_COV!SSE_DEP_BENE_COV_VAL

- TI: `SSE_DEP_BENE_COV_VAL` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_DEP_BENE_COV` → `M4SSE_DEP_BENE_COV`

```sql
SELECT @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = #MAX(A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO), @STD_ID_PERSON = #MAX(A.STD_ID_PERSON) FROM &SSE_DEP_BENE_COV A WHERE (A.ID_ESTADO_REG = 'PENDIENTE') GROUP BY A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO ORDER BY A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO ASC
```

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @SUS_DT_END = H.SUS_DT_END, @STD_N_ACT_DEP_TYPE = S.STD_N_ACT_DEP_TYPE, @SUS_NUM = A.SUS_NUM, @SUS_NUM_SUBVEN = A.SUS_NUM_SUBVEN, @SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE = A.SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE, @SUS_MAX_COV = P.SUS_MAX_COV, @SUS_ID_PLAN = H.SUS_ID_PLAN, @SUS_ID_HR = H.SUS_ID_HR, @SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD = H.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SUS_OR_H_EE_BNFT = H.SUS_OR_H_EE_BNFT, @SUS_DT_START = H.SUS_DT_START, @SUS_N_PLAN = I.SUS_N_PLAN, @SUS_N_OPTION = L.SUS_N_OPTION, @SUS_ID_OPTION = H.SUS_ID_OPTION, @SUS_ID_COV_CAT = H.SUS_ID_COV_CAT, @SUS_N_COV_CAT = P.SUS_N_COV_CAT, @STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE = S.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE, @STD_ID_FAMILY_PERSON = U.STD_ID_FAMILY_PERSON, @SCO_GB_NAME = V.SCO_GB_NAME FROM &SUS_COV_DEP A, SUS_H_EE_IN_BNFT H, SUS_PLAN I, SUS_PLAN_OPTION L, SUS_COV_CAT P, STD_LU_ACT_DEP_TYPE S, STD_LU_DEP_TYPE T, STD_FAMILY U, STD_PERSON V WHERE A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = H.SUS_ID_COV_CAT AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION = I.ID_ORGANIZATION AND H.SUS_ID_PLAN = I.SUS_ID_PLAN AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION = L.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND H.SUS_ID_OPTION = L.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) AND H.SUS_ID_PLAN = L.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = P.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = P.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND A.SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE = S.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE(+) AND T.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = S.ID_ORGANIZATION AND T.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE(+) = S.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE AND U.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = T.ID_ORGANIZATION AND U.STD_ID_DEP_TYPE(+) = T.STD_ID_DEP_TYPE AND U.ID_ORGANIZATION = V.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND U.STD_ID_FAMILY_PERSON = V.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (H.SUS_ID_HR = $ID_PERSON And H.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SSE_P_OR_HR_PERIOD And U.STD_ID_HR = H.SUS_ID_HR And H.SUS_ID_PLAN = @SSE_P_ID_PLAN And H.SUS_OR_H_EE_BNFT = @SSE_P_OR_PLAN And H.SUS_ID_OPTION = @SSE_P_ID_OPTION And H.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = @SSE_P_ID_COV_CAT And ( A.SUS_DT_START <= @SSE_P_DT_START And A.SUS_DT_END >= @SSE_P_DT_END ) And ( A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = @SSE_P_ID_COV_CAT Or @SSE_P_ID_COV_CAT IS NULL )) ORDER BY H.SUS_ID_PLAN ASC, A.SUS_ID_DEP_TYPE ASC, H.SUS_DT_START ASC, H.SUS_ID_HR ASC, H.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD ASC, U.STD_ID_FAMILY_PERSON ASC
```


## SSE_BFT_EE_BNFT_ELEC!M4T_BNFT_AC_PERIOD

- Conector: `SCO_DT_PAYMENT` = `M4T_BNFT_LAST_PAYMENT.SCO_DT_PAYMENT`
- Parámetro 1: `SSE_P_ID_HR`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_DT_PAYMENT = #MAX(A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT), @SSP_BASE_IRPF = #SUM(A.SSP_BASE_IRPF), @CCO_TOT_BNFT_HR_PRE = #SUM(A.CCO_TOT_BNFT_HR_PRE), @SSP_PORC_IRPF = #MAX(A.SSP_PORC_IRPF), @SCO_DT_ALLOCATION = #MAX(A.SCO_DT_ALLOCATION), @SCO_ID_HR = #MAX(A.SCO_ID_HR), @SSP_TOTAL_DEVENGOS = #SUM(A.SSP_TOTAL_DEVENGOS), @ID_CURRENCY = #MAX(A.ID_CURRENCY), @SCO_DT_EXCHANGE = #MAX(A.SCO_DT_EXCHANGE), @EX_TYPE = #MAX(A.EX_TYPE), @SSP_COT_DFPS_IND = #MAX(A.SSP_COT_DFPS_IND), @SSP_COT_REG_GEN_IND = #MAX(A.SSP_COT_RG_GEN_IND), @SSP_LIQUIDO = #SUM(A.SSP_LIQUIDO) FROM &SCO_AC_HR_PERIOD A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_HR = @SSE_P_ID_HR) GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_HR
```

## SSE_BFT_EE_BNFT_ELEC!M4T_BNFT_LAST_PAYMENT

```sql
SELECT @SCO_DT_PAYMENT = #MAX(A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT), @SCO_GB_NAME = #MAX(B.SCO_GB_NAME) FROM &SCO_AC_HR_PERIOD A, STD_PERSON B WHERE A.SCO_ID_HR = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT = A.SCO_DT_ALLOCATION And A.SCO_ID_HR = $ID_PERSON)
```

@SCO_CUR_DATE = T.SCO_CUR_DATE, @SUS_PRICE = V.SUS_PRICE, @SCO_EX_TYPE = T.SCO_EX_TYPE, @SUS_ID_CURRENCY = V.SUS_ID_CURRENCY, @SUS_CUR_DATE = V.SUS_CUR_DATE, @SUS_MIN_COV1 = T.SUS_MIN_COV1, @SUS_EX_TYPE = V.SUS_EX_TYPE, @SUS_MAX_COV1 = T.SUS_MAX_COV1, @SCO_ID_DOC_1 = D.SCO_ID_DOC, @STD_ID_COMP_BASIS = V.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS, @STD_ID_COMP_EC = V.STD_ID_COMP_EC, @SUS_DT_START_PP = H.SUS_DT_START, @STD_N_COMP_BASIS = W.STD_N_COMP_BASIS, @SUS_DT_END_PP = H.SUS_DT_END, @STD_N_COMP_BASIS_EC = X.STD_N_COMP_BASIS, @SUS_FLEX_PLAN = B.SUS_FLEX_PLAN, @SUS_TAX_1 = B.SUS_TAX, @STD_ID_COMP_TP = A.STD_ID_COMP_TP, @SUS_TAX_ELCT_D = A.SUS_TAX_ELCT_D, @STD_ID_COMP_CR = A.STD_ID_COMP_CR, @SUS_WEB_ADD_DESC = B.SUS_WEB_ADD_DESC, @SUS_COVERAGE = A.SUS_COVERAGE, @STD_N_COMP_BASIS_CR = R.STD_N_COMP_BASIS, @SUS_CREDIT = A.SUS_CREDIT, @STD_N_COMP_BASIS_TP = Q.STD_N_COMP_BASIS, @SUS_TOTAL_PREM = A.SUS_TOTAL_PREM, @SUS_SMOKER = A.SUS_SMOKER, @SCO_ID_DOC = B.SCO_ID_DOC, @SUS_ID_PLAN_TYPE = B.SUS_ID_PLAN_TYPE, @STD_N_EXT_ORG = K.STD_N_EXT_ORG, @SCO_HTTP_PATH = K.SCO_HTTP_PATH, @SUS_N_PLAN_PERIOD = H.SUS_N_PLAN_PERIOD, @SUS_OR_BNFT_ELECT = A.SUS_OR_BNFT_ELECT, @SUS_ID_HR = A.SUS_ID_HR, @SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SUS_DT_START = A.SUS_DT_START, @SUS_DT_END = A.SUS_DT_END, @SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD = A.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD, @SUS_ID_OPTION = A.SUS_ID_OPTION, @SUS_ID_COV_CAT = A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT, @STD_ID_COMP_BASIS_C = A.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS_C, @SUS_PCT_PRICE = A.SUS_PCT_PRICE, @SUS_ID_COMP_BASE = A.SUS_ID_COMP_BASE, @SUS_TAX = A.SUS_TAX, @SUS_ELECTED = A.SUS_ELECTED, @SUS_COMMENT = A.SUS_COMMENT, @SUS_N_PLAN = B.SUS_N_PLAN, @SUS_N_PLAN_TYPE = C.SUS_N_PLAN_TYPE, @SUS_N_OPTION = D.SUS_N_OPTION, @STD_DT_START = S.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = S.STD_DT_END, @SUS_ID_ENROL_PRD = U.SUS_ID_ENROL_PRD, @SUS_N_COV_CAT = G.SUS_N_COV_CAT, @SUS_MAX_COV = G.SUS_MAX_COV FROM &SUS_EE_BNFT_ELCT A, SUS_PLAN B, SUS_PLAN_TYPE C, SUS_PLAN_OPTION D, SUS_PLAN_OPT_COV F, SUS_COV_CAT G, SUS_PLAN_PERIOD H, SUS_BNFT_TPAS I, STD_HRM_VENDOR J, STD_EXTERNAL_ORGANIZATION K, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS Q, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS R, STD_HR_PERIOD S, SUS_V_LI_OPT T, SUS_ENROL_PERIOD U, SCO_BN_ELCT_CST V, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS W, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS X WHERE V.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND V.SUS_ID_HR(+) = A.SUS_ID_HR AND V.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) = A.SUS_ID_PLAN AND V.SUS_OR_BNFT_ELECT(+) = A.SUS_OR_BNFT_ELECT AND V.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) = A.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN = B.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND B.SUS_ID_PLAN_TYPE = C.SUS_ID_PLAN_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_OPTION = D.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN = D.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = D.ID_ORGANIZATION AND I.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) = D.SUS_ID_OPTION AND I.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) = D.SUS_ID_PLAN AND T.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) = D.SUS_ID_OPTION AND T.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) = D.SUS_ID_PLAN AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = F.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND A.SUS_ID_OPTION = F.SUS_ID_OPTION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN = F.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.SUS_ID_COV_CAT = G.SUS_ID_COV_CAT(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD = H.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD(+) AND U.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = H.ID_ORGANIZATION AND U.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD(+) = H.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD AND I.STD_ID_HRM_VENDOR = J.STD_ID_HRM_VENDOR(+) AND J.ID_ORGANIZATION = K.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND J.STD_ID_HRM_VENDOR = K.STD_ID_EXTERNAL_ORG(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = Q.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COMP_TP = Q.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = R.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COMP_CR = R.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS(+) AND A.SUS_ID_HR = S.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD = S.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND V.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS = W.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS(+) AND V.STD_ID_COMP_EC = X.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS(+) AND (U.SUS_ID_ENROL_PRD IS NOT NULL And ( #TODAY () >= U.SUS_DT_START And #TODAY () <= U.SUS_DT_END ) And V.SCO_DT_START (+) <= @SCO_TODAY And V.SCO_DT_END (+) >= @SCO_TODAY) ORDER BY A.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD ASC, H.SUS_DT_START DESC, A.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD ASC, B.SUS_ID_PLAN_TYPE ASC, A.SUS_ID_PLAN ASC, A.SUS_ID_OPTION ASC, A.SUS_ID_COV_CAT ASC

A.SUS_CREDIT, @STD_N_COMP_BASIS_CR = C.STD_N_COMP_BASIS, @SUS_ER_CONTR = A.SUS_ER_CONTR, @STD_N_COMP_BASIS_EC = E.STD_N_COMP_BASIS, @SUS_INCOME_W = A.SUS_INCOME_W, @SUS_POST_AN_LIMT = @SUS_DT_END = A.SUS_DT_END, @SUS_MAIN_RL_DEDUC = A.SUS_MAIN_RL_DEDUC, @SUS_PERIOD_1ST = A.SUS_PERIOD_1ST, @SUS_PERIOD_2ND = A.SUS_PERIOD_2ND, @SUS_PERIOD_3RD = A.SUS_PERIOD_3RD, @SUS_PERIOD_4TH = A.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS_W, @SUS_ID_DEDUCTION = A.SUS_ID_DEDUCTION, @SUS_PRIORITY = A.SUS_PRIORITY, @SUS_ID_ARREAR = A.SUS_ID_ARREAR, @SUS_COMMENT = A.SUS_COMMENT, @SUS_PLAN_HC_IND = A.SUS_PLAN_HC_IND, @SUS_PLAN_DIS_IND = A.SUS_PLAN_DIS_IND FROM &SUS_H_EE_IN_BNFT A, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS B, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS C, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS E, STD_LU_COMP_BASIS F, STD_HR_PERIOD G, SUS_PLAN_PERIOD I, SUS_PLAN J A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COMP_EC = E.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COMP_TP = F.STD_ID_COMP_BASIS(+) AND A.SUS_ID_HR = G.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.SUS_OR_HR_PERIOD = G.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD = I.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = @SSE_ID_COMP_BASE = A.SSE_ID_COMP_BASE, @SSE_SMOKER = A.SSE_SMOKER, @SSE_TAX = A.SSE_TAX, @SSE_ID_DEDUCT = A.SSE_ID_DEDUCT, @SSE_ID_COMP_BASIS = A.SSE_ID_COMP_BASIS, @SSE_PCT_ER_CONT = A.SSE_PCT_ER_CONT, @SSE_ID_COMP_BASIS_W = A.SSE_ID_COMP_BASIS_W, @SSE_ID_DEDUCTION = A.SSE_ID_DEDUCTION, @SSE_PRIORITY = A.SSE_PRIORITY, @SSE_ID_ARREAR = A.SSE_ID_ARREAR, @SSE_COMMENT = A.SSE_COMMENT, SUS_BNFT_TPAS E, STD_HRM_VENDOR F, STD_EXTERNAL_ORGANIZATION G, SSE_ACCIONES H, STD_HR_PERIOD I WHERE A.SSE_ID_PLAN = B.SUS_ID_PLAN(+) AND A.SSE_ID_PLAN_PERIOD = C.SUS_ID_PLAN_PERIOD(+) AND E.STD_ID_HRM_VENDOR = F.STD_ID_HRM_VENDOR(+) AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.STD_ID_HRM_VENDOR = G.STD_ID_EXTERNAL_ORG(+) AND A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = H.ACCION_ACEPTADO(+) AND ## SSE_CR_PREFERENC!M4T_CR_PREFERENC

- TI: `M4T_CR_PREFERENC` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_CR_PREFERENC` → `M4SCO_CR_PREFERENC`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_TODAY`
- Parámetro 2: `SCO_TODAY`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SCO_PREFERENCES = A.SCO_PREFERENCES, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @SCO_CK_DISCART_PREFER = A.SCO_CK_DISCART_PREFER, @SCO_OR_PREFER = A.SCO_OR_PREFER, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT, @STD_N_COUNTRY = B.STD_N_COUNTRY, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = C.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_GEO_DIV = D.STD_N_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV = E.STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = F.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_CK_NAT_INT = A.SCO_CK_NAT_INT, @STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_COUNTRY = A.STD_ID_COUNTRY, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @STD_ID_JOB_CODE = A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @SCO_PREF_PRIORITY = A.SCO_PREF_PRIORITY FROM &SCO_CR_PREFERENC A, STD_COUNTRY B, STD_JOB C, STD_GEO_DIV D, STD_SUB_GEO_DIV E, STD_WORK_UNIT F, STD_HR_PERIOD G WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = B.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE = C.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = D.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = D.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = E.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = E.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = E.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = F.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_HR = G.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = G.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND (( A.SCO_CK_DISCART_PREFER IS NULL Or A.SCO_CK_DISCART_PREFER <> '1' ) And G.STD_DT_START <= @SCO_TODAY And G.STD_DT_END >= @SCO_TODAY) ORDER BY A.SCO_PREF_PRIORITY ASC
```

- TI: `SSE_CR_PREFERENC` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_CR_PREFERENC` → `M4SSE_CR_PREFERENC`

```sql
SELECT @ORDINAL = A.ORDINAL, @SCO_PREFERENCES = A.SCO_PREFERENCES, @STD_N_GEO_DIV = G.STD_N_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV = H.STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV, @SCO_OR_PREFER = A.SCO_OR_PREFER, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = C.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = E.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @STD_N_COUNTRY = F.STD_N_COUNTRY, @N_ACCION = B.N_ACCION, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO, @MAIL_EMPLEADO = A.MAIL_EMPLEADO, @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE2 = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE2, @ACCION_ACEPTADO = A.ACCION_ACEPTADO, @NIVEL_ACEPTADO = A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO, @MOTIVO_ACCION = A.MOTIVO_ACCION, @MAIL_BATCH = A.MAIL_BATCH, @PERSIST_BATCH = A.PERSIST_BATCH, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @SCO_PREF_PRIORITY = A.SCO_PREF_PRIORITY, @SCO_CK_NAT_INT = A.SCO_CK_NAT_INT, @STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_COUNTRY = A.STD_ID_COUNTRY, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @STD_ID_JOB_CODE = A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE FROM &SSE_CR_PREFERENC A, SSE_ACCIONES B, STD_JOB C, STD_WORK_UNIT E, STD_COUNTRY F, STD_GEO_DIV G, STD_SUB_GEO_DIV H WHERE A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = B.ACCION_ACEPTADO(+) AND A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE = C.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = E.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = F.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = G.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = G.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = H.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = H.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = H.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND (A.ID_ESTADO_REG = 'PENDIENTE') ORDER BY A.ORDINAL DESC
```

- TI: `M4T_EMP_CV_BACKGROUND` · objeto de lectura: `STD_HR_ACAD_BACKGROUND` → `STD_HR_ACAD_BACKGR`
- Parámetro 1: `ID_PERSONA`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_N_EDU_SP = L.STD_N_EDU_SP, @STD_COMMENT = A.STD_COMMENT, @STD_ID_EDU_CENTER = A.STD_ID_EDU_CENTER, @STD_DESC_EDU_CENTER = A.STD_DESC_EDU_CENTER, @STD_N_EDU_TYPE = K.STD_N_EDU_TYPE, @STD_DT_EARNED_EXPE = A.STD_DT_EARNED_EXPE, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_N_DIP_LEVEL = H.STD_N_DIP_LEVEL, @STD_N_EXT_ORG = C.STD_N_EXT_ORG, @STD_N_DIPLOMA = E.STD_N_DIPLOMA FROM &STD_HR_ACAD_BACKGROUND A, SCO_EDUC_CENTER B, STD_EXTERNAL_ORGANIZATION C, STD_LU_EDU_DIPLOMA E, STD_LU_EDU_DIP_LEVEL H, STD_LU_EDU_TYPE K, STD_LU_EDU_SPECIALITY L WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_CENTER = B.SCO_ID_EDUC_CENTER(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_EDUC_CENTER = C.STD_ID_EXTERNAL_ORG(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_DIPLOMA = E.STD_ID_DIPLOMA(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND E.STD_ID_DIP_LEVEL = H.STD_ID_DIP_LEVEL(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = K.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE = K.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = L.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_SP = L.STD_ID_EDU_SP(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE = L.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE(+) AND (A.STD_ID_HR = @ID_PERSONA) ORDER BY A.STD_DT_START DESC
```

## SSE_EMP_CV!M4T_EMP_CV_LANGUAGES

- TI: `M4T_EMP_CV_LANGUAGES` · objeto de lectura: `STD_HR_LANG` → `STD_HR_LANG`
- Parámetro 1: `ID_PERSONA`

```sql
SELECT @STD_N_LANGUAGE = B.STD_N_LANGUAGE, @STD_N_LANG_LEVEL = D.STD_N_LANG_LEVEL, @STD_N_LANG_LEVEL_1 = E.STD_N_LANG_LEVEL, @STD_N_LANG_LEVEL_2 = F.STD_N_LANG_LEVEL FROM &STD_HR_LANG A, STD_LU_LANGUAGES B, STD_HT_HR_LANG C, STD_LU_LANG_LEVEL D, STD_LU_LANG_LEVEL E, STD_LU_LANG_LEVEL F WHERE C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND C.STD_ID_HR(+) = A.STD_ID_HR AND C.STD_ID_LANGUAGE(+) = A.STD_ID_LANGUAGE AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_LANGUAGE = B.STD_ID_LANGUAGE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_SPEAK_LEVEL = D.STD_ID_LANG_LEVEL(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_LISTEN_LEVEL = E.STD_ID_LANG_LEVEL(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_WRITE_LEVEL = F.STD_ID_LANG_LEVEL(+) AND (A.STD_ID_HR = @ID_PERSONA And ( A.DT_END = C.STD_DT_END Or C.STD_DT_END = #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) )) ORDER BY A.DT_START DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_INITIAL_JOB = A.STD_INITIAL_JOB, @STD_FINAL_JOB = A.STD_FINAL_JOB, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = A.STD_DT_END, @STD_EMPLOYER = A.STD_EMPLOYER, @STD_DEVELOPED_ACTIVITIES = A.STD_DEVELOPED_ACTIVITIES, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT, @STD_N_SECTOR = B.STD_N_SECTOR FROM &STD_HR_PREV_JOBS A, STD_LU_JOB_SECTOR B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_SECTOR = B.STD_ID_SECTOR(+) AND (A.STD_ID_HR = @ID_PERSONA) ORDER BY A.STD_DT_START DESC

```


```sql
SELECT @ORDINAL = A.ORDINAL, @MAIL_EMPLEADO = A.MAIL_EMPLEADO, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE2 = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE2, @ACCION_ACEPTADO = A.ACCION_ACEPTADO, @MOTIVO_ACCION = A.MOTIVO_ACCION, @NIVEL_ACEPTADO = A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO, @PERSIST_BATCH = A.PERSIST_BATCH, @MAIL_BATCH = A.MAIL_BATCH, @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG, @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_ID_LANGUAGE = A.STD_ID_LANGUAGE, @STD_ID_SPEAK_LEVEL = A.STD_ID_SPEAK_LEVEL, @STD_ID_LISTEN_LEVEL = A.STD_ID_LISTEN_LEVEL, @STD_ID_WRITE_LEVEL = A.STD_ID_WRITE_LEVEL, @MOTIVO_CANCELACION = A.MOTIVO_CANCELACION, @N_ACCION = B.N_ACCION FROM &SSE_EMP_LANG A, SSE_ACCIONES B WHERE A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = B.ACCION_ACEPTADO(+) AND (A.ID_ESTADO_REG = 'PENDIENTE') ORDER BY A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO ASC, A.ORDINAL ASC
```

- Parámetro 4: `PROP_ID_STATUS`

```sql

H.SCO_WKNG_HRS_DAY, @SCO_NM_DAY_TYPE_4 = I.SCO_NM_DAY_TYPE, @SCO_WKNG_HRS_DAY_4 = I.SCO_WKNG_HRS_DAY, @SCO_NM_DAY_TYPE_5 = J.SCO_NM_DAY_TYPE, @SCO_WKNG_HRS_DAY_5 = J.SCO_WKNG_HRS_DAY, @SCO_NM_DAY_TYPE_6 = K.SCO_NM_DAY_TYPE, @SCO_WKNG_HRS_DAY_6 = K.SCO_WKNG_HRS_DAY, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @SCO_OR_WEEK = A.SCO_OR_WEEK, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT, @SCO_NM_WEEK = C.SCO_NM_WEEK, @SCO_NM_ENTER_TYPE = D.SCO_NM_ENTER_TYPE, @SCO_DAY_MONDAY = C.SCO_DAY_MONDAY, @SCO_DAY_TUESDAY = C.SCO_DAY_TUESDAY, @SCO_DAY_WEDNESDAY = C.SCO_DAY_WEDNESDAY, @SCO_DAY_THURSDAY = C.SCO_DAY_THURSDAY, @SCO_DAY_FRIDAY = C.SCO_DAY_FRIDAY, @SCO_DAY_SATURDAY = C.SCO_DAY_SATURDAY, @SCO_DAY_SUNDAY = C.SCO_DAY_SUNDAY, @SCO_TOT_WKNG_HRS = C.SCO_TOT_WKNG_HRS, @SCO_TOT_WKNG_DAYS = C.SCO_TOT_WKNG_DAYS FROM &SCO_HW_MODEL A, SCO_WEEK C, SCO_ENTER_TYPE D, SCO_DAY_TYPE K, SCO_DAY_TYPE J, SCO_DAY_TYPE I, SCO_DAY_TYPE H, SCO_DAY_TYPE G, SCO_DAY_TYPE F, SCO_DAY_TYPE E, SCO_WEEKLY_MODEL L WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_OR_WEEK = C.SCO_OR_WEEK(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_ENTER_TYPE = D.SCO_ID_ENTER_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = K.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_DAY_THURSDAY = K.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = J.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_DAY_SUNDAY = J.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_DAY_SATURDAY = I.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_DAY_WEDNESDAY = H.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_DAY_FRIDAY = G.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_DAY_MONDAY = F.SCO_ID_DAY_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) SELECT @SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN, @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_WEIGHT = A.SCO_WEIGHT, @SCO_NM_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_NM_EXTD_KN, @SCO_NM_LEVEL = D.SCO_NM_LEVEL FROM &SCO_H_HR_KNC_LVL A, SCO_KNOW_MAP B, SCO_KNOW_LEVEL C, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL D WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_1 = C.SCO_DT_START(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = C.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = C.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_LEVEL = D.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND C.SCO_ID_SCALE = D.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND (A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And ( A.SCO_DT_END IS NULL Or #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END ) And A.STD_ID_HR = @SMCO_HR_TO_FILTER)
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_FIX_SALARY_REAL = A.SCO_FIX_SALARY_REAL, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = E.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @SCO_VAR_SALARY_REAL = A.SCO_VAR_SALARY_REAL, @SCO_GB_NAME = E.SCO_GB_NAME, @NM_CURRENCY = B.NM_CURRENCY, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_FIX_SALARY = A.SCO_FIX_SALARY, @ID_CURRENCY = A.ID_CURRENCY, @SCO_DT_EXCHANGE = A.SCO_DT_EXCHANGE, @EX_TYPE = A.EX_TYPE, @SCO_VAR_SALARY = A.SCO_VAR_SALARY, @SCO_FIX_SALARY_PER = A.SCO_FIX_SALARY_PER, @SCO_VAR_SALARY_PER = A.SCO_VAR_SALARY_PER, @SCO_COT_EMP = A.SCO_COT_EMP, @SCO_BNFT_LEG_ENT = A.SCO_BNFT_LEG_ENT, @SCO_BNFT_LEG_PER = A.SCO_BNFT_LEG_PER FROM &SCO_H_SAL_DATA A, SCH_CURRENCY B, STD_HR_PERIOD C, STD_HR D, STD_PERSON E WHERE A.ID_CURRENCY = B.ID_CURRENCY(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = C.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = C.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_HR = D.STD_ID_HR(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND D.STD_ID_HR = E.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_HR = @SMCO_HR_TO_FILTER) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC
```

- Parámetro 3: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 4: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 5: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 6: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS = B.SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS, @SCO_FULL_TIME = B.SCO_FULL_TIME, @SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD = B.SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD FROM &SCO_HR_ROLE A, SCO_H_HR_ROLE_TIME B WHERE B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND B.SCO_ID_HR(+) = A.SCO_ID_HR AND B.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND (A.SCO_ID_HR = @SMCO_HR_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SMCO_HR_OR_PERIOD And A.SCO_DT_START <= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_DT_END >= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_MAIN_ROLE = '1' And B.SCO_DT_START <= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER And B.SCO_DT_END >= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER)
```

## SSE_HOLYDAYS!M4T_REAL_TIME_PRD

- TI: `M4T_REAL_TIME_PRD` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_REAL_TIME_PRD` → `M4SCO_REAL_TM_PRD`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SCO_NM_TIME_UNIT = C.SCO_NM_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_NM_INCIDENCE = B.SCO_NM_INCIDENCE, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ID_INCIDENCE = A.SCO_ID_INCIDENCE, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_OR_PART_TIME = A.SCO_OR_PART_TIME, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_START_TIME = A.SCO_START_TIME, @SCO_END_TIME = A.SCO_END_TIME, @SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT, @SCO_UNITS = A.SCO_UNITS, @SCO_LAST_DAY_PROCESS = A.SCO_LAST_DAY_PROCESS, @SCO_CK_IN_PAYROLL = A.SCO_CK_IN_PAYROLL, @SCO_ID_INP_PAYPERIOD = A.SCO_ID_INP_PAYPERIOD, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT, @SSP_PORC_MAT_PRC = A.SSP_PORC_MAT_PRC FROM &SCO_REAL_TIME_PRD A, SCO_INCIDENCE B, SCO_X_TIME_UNIT C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_INCIDENCE = B.SCO_ID_INCIDENCE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT = C.SCO_ID_TIME_UNIT(+) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_DT_START = #MAX(A.SCO_DT_START), @SCO_NUM_MAX_CFWD = #SUM(C.SCO_NUM_MAX_CFWD), @SCO_ID_INCIDENCE = B.SCO_ID_INCIDENCE, @SCO_DT_END = #MIN(A.SCO_DT_END), @SCO_NUM_ENTITLEMENT = #SUM(A.SCO_NUM_ENTITLEMENT), @SCO_NUM_ENTITLEMENT_N1 = #SUM(A.SCO_NUM_ENTITLEMENT_N1), @SCO_NUM_CARRIED_FORWARD = #SUM(A.SCO_NUM_CARRIED_FORWARD), @SCO_NUM_MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT = #SUM(A.SCO_NUM_MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT), @SCO_TOT_REMAINING = #SUM(A.SCO_TOT_REMAINING) FROM &SCO_AB_H_HRP_ENT_SUMMARY A, SCO_X_AB_ENTITLEMENT_TYPE B, SCO_AB_SCHEME C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION AND A.SCO_ID_ENTITLEMENT_TYPE = B.SCO_ID_ENTITLEMENT_TYPE AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_SCHEME = C.SCO_ID_SCHEME(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_HR = $ID_PERSON) GROUP BY B.SCO_ID_INCIDENCE ORDER BY B.SCO_ID_INCIDENCE ASC
```

```sql

```


```sql
SELECT @ORDINAL = A.ORDINAL, @MAIL_EMPLEADO = A.MAIL_EMPLEADO, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE2 = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE2, @ACCION_ACEPTADO = A.ACCION_ACEPTADO, @MOTIVO_ACCION = A.MOTIVO_ACCION, @NIVEL_ACEPTADO = A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO, @PERSIST_BATCH = A.PERSIST_BATCH, @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG, @MAIL_BATCH = A.MAIL_BATCH, @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @SSE_SOLIC_DATE = A.SSE_SOLIC_DATE, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT, @N_ACCION = B.N_ACCION, @SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT = A.SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT, @SCO_ID_JOB = C.SCO_ID_JOB, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = D.STD_N_JOB_CODE FROM &SSE_INT_MOVILITY A, SSE_ACCIONES B, SCO_RECRUIT_PRO C, STD_JOB D WHERE A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = B.ACCION_ACEPTADO(+) AND A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = C.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_JOB = D.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND (A.ID_ESTADO_REG = 'PENDIENTE') ORDER BY A.ORDINAL DESC

```

- TI: `SSE_JOB_COMPETENCY` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_R_JOB_COMP` → `M4SCO_R_JOB_COMP`

```sql
SELECT @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @SCO_ID_JOB = A.SCO_ID_JOB, @SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @SCO_WEIGHT = A.SCO_WEIGHT, @SCO_NM_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_NM_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = C.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_MEANING = C.SCO_MEANING FROM &SCO_R_JOB_COMP A, SCO_KNOW_MAP B, SCO_KNOW_LEVEL C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = B.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_1 = C.SCO_DT_START(+) AND A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = C.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = C.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND (( #TODAY () >= A.DT_START And #TODAY () <= A.DT_END ) Or A.DT_END IS NULL) ORDER BY A.SCO_WEIGHT ASC, B.SCO_NM_EXTD_KN ASC
```




```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @SSP_ID_TP_DOC = A.SSP_ID_TP_DOC, @STD_OR_DEP_NB = A.STD_OR_DEP_NB, @STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE = A.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE, @STD_DT_START = A.STD_DT_START, @STD_DT_END = A.STD_DT_END, @SSP_SSN_SPOUSE = A.SSP_SSN_SPOUSE FROM &SSP_DEPEND_IRPF A WHERE (A.STD_ID_ACT_DEP_TYPE = '1' And A.STD_ID_HR = $ID_PERSON And A.STD_DT_START <= @SSP_FEC_CORTE And A.STD_DT_END >= @SSP_FEC_CORTE)
```

- Parámetro 2: `SSP_FEC_CORTE`


```sql
SELECT @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_NM_LEVEL = F.SCO_NM_LEVEL, @SCO_ACCOMP_VALUE = A.SCO_ACCOMP_VALUE, @SCO_WEIGHT = A.SCO_WEIGHT, @SCO_SCHED_VALUE = A.SCO_SCHED_VALUE, @SCO_NM_MAGNITUDE = D.SCO_NM_MAGNITUDE, @SCO_N_ROLE = G.SCO_N_ROLE, @SCO_NM_OBJECTIVE = B.SCO_NM_OBJECTIVE FROM &SCO_ROL_LV_OBJ A, SCO_OBJECTIVE B, SCO_X_OBJ_MAGN D, SCO_OBJ_LEVEL E, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL F, SCO_HR_ROLE G WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE = B.SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_MAGNITUD = D.SCO_ID_MAGNITUD(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START1 = E.SCO_DT_START(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = E.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND A.SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE = E.SCO_ID_OBJECTIVE(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND E.SCO_ID_LEVEL = F.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND E.SCO_ID_SCALE = F.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = G.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = G.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND (( #TODAY () >= A.SCO_DT_START And ( #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END Or A.SCO_DT_END IS NULL ) ) And @SSE_ID_HR_PAR = A.SCO_ID_HR) ORDER BY A.SCO_WEIGHT DESC
```

- TI: `M4T_OTHER_PDATA` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_PAYMENT_DATA` → `M4SCO_PAYMENT_DATA`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE, @SCO_ACCOUNT_NUMBER_1 = F.SCO_ACCOUNT_NUMBER, @SCO_NM_PAYMFORMULA_1 = E.SCO_NM_PAYMFORMULA, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_EMP_CHECK = A.SCO_EMP_CHECK, @SCO_ID_PERSON = A.SCO_ID_PERSON, @SCO_ID_EXTERNALORG = A.SCO_ID_EXTERNALORG, @SCO_OR_ACCOUNT = A.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT, @SCO_ID_PAYM_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_PAYM_TYPE, @SCO_ID_PAY_FORMULA = A.SCO_ID_PAY_FORMULA, @STD_N_EXT_ORG_1 = J.STD_N_EXT_ORG, @SCO_ID_COMP_BANK = A.SCO_ID_COMP_BANK, @SCO_LE_CB_CHECK = A.SCO_LE_CB_CHECK, @SSP_LITERAL_REG_7 = A.SSP_LITERAL_REG_7, @ID_CURRENCY_DATA = A.ID_CURRENCY_DATA, @SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA = A.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA, @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = B.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @SCO_GB_NAME = I.SCO_GB_NAME, @STD_N_FIRST_NAME = B.STD_N_FIRST_NAME, @SSP_PAY_FORM_TP = E.SSP_PAY_FORM_TP FROM &SCO_PAYMENT_DATA A, STD_PERSON B, SCO_PAYM_FORMULA E, SCO_PERSON_BANK F, STD_HR_PERIOD G, STD_HR H, STD_PERSON I, STD_EXTERNAL_ORGANIZATION J WHERE A.SCO_ID_PERSON = B.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_PAY_FORMULA = E.SCO_ID_PAY_FORMULA(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_PERSON = F.SCO_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT = F.SCO_OR_ACCOUNT(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = G.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = G.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND G.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND G.STD_ID_HR = H.STD_ID_HR(+) AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION = I.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND H.STD_ID_HR = I.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = J.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EXTERNALORG = J.STD_ID_EXTERNAL_ORG(+)
```

- Conector: `SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE` = `M4T_OTHER_PDATA.SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE`
- Conector: `SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD` = `M4T_OTHER_PDATA.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD`
- Conector: `SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA` = `M4T_OTHER_PDATA.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA = A.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA, @SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM = A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM, @SCO_DT_PAYMENT = A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT, @SCO_PAY_FREQ_ALLOC = A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_ALLOC, @SCO_DT_ALLOCATION = A.SCO_DT_ALLOCATION, @SCO_DT_END = #MAX(B.SCO_DT_END) FROM &SCO_PAYMEN_ORDER A, SCO_HT_PAYS B WHERE A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT = B.SCO_DT_ACCRUED AND A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM = B.SCO_ID_PAY_FREQUENCY AND (B.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY ()) GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE, A.SCO_ID_HR, A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, A.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA, A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM, A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT, A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_ALLOC, A.SCO_DT_ALLOCATION
```

- Conector: `SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD` = `M4T_PAYMENT_DATA.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD`
- Conector: `SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA` = `M4T_PAYMENT_DATA.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA = A.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA, @SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM = A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM, @SCO_DT_PAYMENT = A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT, @SCO_PAY_FREQ_ALLOC = A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_ALLOC, @SCO_DT_ALLOCATION = A.SCO_DT_ALLOCATION, @SCO_DT_END = #MAX(B.SCO_DT_END) FROM &SCO_PAYMEN_ORDER A, SCO_HT_PAYS B WHERE A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT = B.SCO_DT_ACCRUED AND A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM = B.SCO_ID_PAY_FREQUENCY AND (B.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY ()) GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_ORIGIN_TYPE, A.SCO_ID_HR, A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, A.SCO_OR_PAYMENTDATA, A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_PAYM, A.SCO_DT_PAYMENT, A.SCO_PAY_FREQ_ALLOC, A.SCO_DT_ALLOCATION

```

- TI: `M4T_H_EVALUATE_2` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_EVALUATE` → `M4SCO_H_EVALUATE`
- Parámetro 1: `FILTRO_JOB`
- Parámetro 2: `FILTRO_JOB`
- Parámetro 3: `FILTRO_WU`
- Parámetro 4: `FILTRO_WU`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_DT_START_EVAL = A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL, @SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = C.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START_PROC = A.SCO_DT_START_PROC, @SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC = D.SCO_NM_EVAL_PROC, @SCO_GB_NAME = E.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_N_ROLE = F.SCO_N_ROLE, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = F.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD FROM &SCO_H_EVALUATE A, SCO_H_HR_WUNIT C, SCO_H_EVAL_PROC D, STD_PERSON E, SCO_HR_ROLE F WHERE A.SCO_ID_HR = C.SCO_ID_HR AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = C.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION AND A.SCO_DT_START_PROC = D.SCO_DT_START_PROC AND A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = D.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN AND A.SCO_ID_HR = E.STD_ID_PERSON AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = F.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = F.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND (( A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = @FILTRO_JOB Or @FILTRO_JOB = 'XXX01' ) And ( C.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = @FILTRO_WU Or @FILTRO_WU = 'XXX01' ) And ( A.SCO_DT_END IS NOT NULL And A.SCO_DT_END <> #DATE ('4000-01-01' ) ) And ( C.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And C.SCO_DT_END > #TODAY () )) ORDER BY A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN ASC, A.SCO_ID_HR ASC
```


```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_PERSON = #MAX(A.STD_ID_PERSON), @SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE = #MAX(E.SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE), @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION = #MAX(B.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION), @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION_1 = #MAX(B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION), @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = #MAX(C.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT), @DT_START = #MAX(B.DT_START), @DT_END = #MAX(B.DT_END) FROM &SCO_ENROLLMENT A, SCO_SUBACTION B, SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT C, SCO_X_DEV_ACT_TYPE E WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE = E.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE(+) AND (A.STD_ID_PERSON = $ID_PERSON) GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, A.STD_ID_PERSON
```

G.SCO_NM_DEV_PRO_TYPE, @SCO_NM_TYPE = D.SCO_NM_TYPE, @MAIL_EMPLEADO = A.MAIL_EMPLEADO, @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = F.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE, @MAIL_RESPONSABLE2 = A.MAIL_RESPONSABLE2, @ACCION_ACEPTADO = A.ACCION_ACEPTADO, @NIVEL_ACEPTADO = A.NIVEL_ACEPTADO, @PERSIST_BATCH = A.PERSIST_BATCH, @MAIL_BATCH = A.MAIL_BATCH, @ID_ESTADO_REG = A.ID_ESTADO_REG, @NOMBRE_EMPLEADO = A.NOMBRE_EMPLEADO, @STD_ID_PERSON = A.STD_ID_PERSON, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP, @STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2 = A.STD_ID_PERSON_RESP2, @SCO_ID_TRTBREQ = A.SCO_ID_TRTBREQ, @SCO_ID_REQUEST = A.SCO_ID_REQUEST, @SCO_ID_TYPE = A.SCO_ID_TYPE, @SCO_ID_TRAINING = A.SCO_ID_TRAINING, @SCO_NM_TRAINING = A.SCO_NM_TRAINING, @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_ID_REQUEST_ORIGIN = A.SCO_ID_REQUEST_ORIGIN, @STD_ID_JOB_CODE = A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE, @STD_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_NUM_PLACES = A.SCO_NUM_PLACES, @SCO_ID_IMP_LV = A.SCO_ID_IMP_LV, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_ID_REQ_STATUS = A.SCO_ID_REQ_STATUS, @STD_ID_LANGUAGE = A.STD_ID_LANGUAGE, @SCO_SD_PREF = A.SCO_SD_PREF, @SCO_ED_PREF = A.SCO_ED_PREF, @SCO_ID_FINANCE_METH = A.SCO_ID_FINANCE_METH, @SCO_OR_PERSON = A.SCO_OR_PERSON, @MOTIVO_CANCELACION = A.MOTIVO_CANCELACION, @SCO_DESCRIPTION = A.SCO_DESCRIPTION, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT FROM &SSE_TRAINING_REQUEST A, SCO_TRTB_REQ B, SCO_TRAIN_TYPE D, SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT F, SCO_X_DEV_PRO_TYPE G, SSE_ACCIONES H WHERE A.SCO_ID_TRTBREQ = B.SCO_ID_TRTBREQ(+) AND A.SCO_ID_TYPE = D.SCO_ID_TYPE(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = F.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND F.SCO_ID_DEV_PRO_TYPE = G.SCO_ID_DEV_PRO_TYPE(+) AND A.ACCION_ACEPTADO = H.ACCION_ACEPTADO(+) AND (A.ID_ESTADO_REG = 'PENDIENTE') ORDER BY A.ORDINAL DESC

- TI: `SSM_CAREER_PLAN` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_CR_PLAN_HT` → `M4SCO_CR_PLAN_HT`

```sql
SELECT @ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = L.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_GB_NAME = J.SCO_GB_NAME, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @STD_ID_HR = A.STD_ID_HR, @STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = C.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @STD_N_FIRST_NAME = C.STD_N_FIRST_NAME, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = F.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @SCO_ID_CR_PATH = A.SCO_ID_CR_PATH, @CAL_OR_HR_PERIOD = (#TO_CHAR (A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD )), @SCO_NM_CAREER_PLAN = A.SCO_NM_CAREER_PLAN FROM &SCO_CR_PLAN_HT A, STD_PERSON C, STD_HR_PERIOD D, SCO_HR_ROLE E, SCO_H_HR_JOB F, STD_HR H, STD_PERSON J, SCO_VW_H_HRR_JOB K, STD_JOB L WHERE A.STD_ID_HR = C.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_HR = D.STD_ID_HR(+) AND A.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD = D.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = D.ID_ORGANIZATION AND E.SCO_ID_HR(+) = D.STD_ID_HR AND E.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD(+) = D.STD_OR_HR_PERIOD AND F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = E.ID_ORGANIZATION AND F.SCO_ID_HR(+) = E.SCO_ID_HR AND F.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND K.SCO_ID_HR(+) = E.SCO_ID_HR AND K.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = E.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = H.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND D.STD_ID_HR = H.STD_ID_HR(+) AND H.ID_ORGANIZATION = J.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND H.STD_ID_HR = J.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND K.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = L.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND (E.SCO_MAIN_ROLE = '1' And D.STD_DT_START <= #TODAY () And D.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () And E.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And E.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY ()) ORDER BY C.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 ASC, C.STD_N_FIRST_NAME ASC
```

- TI: `M4T_H_EVALUATE_1` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_EVALUATE` → `M4SCO_H_EVALUATE`

- Parámetro 4: `FILTRO_WU`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_DT_START_EVAL = A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL, @SCO_ID_EVALUATOR = B.SCO_ID_EVALUATOR, @SCO_OR_EVALUATOR = B.SCO_OR_EVALUATOR, @SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = C.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN, @SCO_DT_START_PROC = A.SCO_DT_START_PROC, @SCO_DT_START_CRI = D.SCO_DT_START_CRI, @SCO_DT_END_CRI = D.SCO_DT_END_CRI FROM &SCO_H_EVALUATE A, SCO_EVALUATOR B, SCO_H_HR_WUNIT C, SCO_H_EVAL_PROC D WHERE B.ID_ORGANIZATION = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND B.SCO_DT_START_EVAL = A.SCO_DT_START_EVAL AND B.SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR AND B.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND A.SCO_ID_HR = C.SCO_ID_HR AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = C.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_PROC = D.SCO_DT_START_PROC(+) AND A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN = D.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN(+) AND (( A.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = @FILTRO_JOB Or @FILTRO_JOB = 'XXX01' ) And ( C.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = @FILTRO_WU Or @FILTRO_WU = 'XXX01' ) And ( C.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And C.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () )) ORDER BY D.SCO_DT_START_CRI DESC, A.SCO_ID_EVAL_PLAN ASC
```

- TI: `M4T_DS` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_TRTB_REQ` → `M4SCO_TRTB_REQ`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_TRTBREQ = A.SCO_ID_TRTBREQ, @SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE = F.SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION = C.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION, @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = D.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT, @SFR_CK_DEDUCTIBLE = C.SFR_CK_DEDUCTIBLE, @SCO_NUMBER_OF_UNITS = C.SCO_NUMBER_OF_UNITS, @SCO_ID_TRAINING_LOCATION_TYPE = C.SCO_ID_TRAINING_LOCATION_TYPE, @SCO_DAYS = C.SCO_DAYS, @SCO_HOURS = C.SCO_HOURS, @SCO_HOURS_OTW = C.SCO_HOURS_OTW, @SCO_NM_SUBACTION_STATUS = G.SCO_NM_SUBACTION_STATUS, @SCO_NM_TRAINING_LOCATION_TYPE = E.SCO_NM_TRAINING_LOCATION_TYPE FROM &SCO_TRTB_REQ A, SCO_SUBACTION C, SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT D, SCO_TRAINING_LOCATION_TYPE E, SCO_X_DEV_ACT_TYPE F, SCO_X_SUBACT_ST G WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = D.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_TRAINING_LOCATION_TYPE = E.SCO_ID_TRAINING_LOCATION_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE = F.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_SUBACTION_STATUS = G.SCO_ID_SUBACTION_STATUS(+)
```

## SSM_ENROLLMENT_OVERVIEW!M4T_ENROLLMENT

- TI: `M4T_ENROLLMENT` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_ENROLLMENT` → `M4SCO_ENROLLMENT`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_TRTBREQ = #MAX(G.SCO_ID_TRTBREQ), @SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE = #MAX(F.SCO_NM_DEV_ACT_TYPE), @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = #MAX(E.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBPRODUCT), @SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = #MAX(A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION), @SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION = #MAX(C.SCO_NM_DEV_SUBACTION), @SCO_DATE = #MAX(D.SCO_DATE), @SCO_DATE_1 = #MIN(D.SCO_DATE), @STD_ID_PERSON = #MAX(A.STD_ID_PERSON) FROM &SCO_ENROLLMENT A, SCO_SUBACTION C, SCO_SUBACTION_CALENDAR D, SCO_DEVEL_SUBPRODUCT E, SCO_X_DEV_ACT_TYPE F, SCO_TRTB_REQ G WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = C.ID_ORGANIZATION AND D.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION AND G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = C.ID_ORGANIZATION AND G.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION(+) = C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT = E.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBPRODUCT(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE = F.SCO_ID_DEV_ACT_TYPE(+) GROUP BY A.SCO_ID_DEV_SUBACTION, A.STD_ID_PERSON
```

- TI: `SMCO_HR_ROLE_CALCULATION` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_HR_ROLE` → `M4SCO_HR_ROLE`
- Parámetro 1: `SMCO_HR_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 2: `SMCO_HR_OR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 3: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 4: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END FROM &SCO_HR_ROLE A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_HR = @SMCO_HR_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SMCO_HR_OR_PERIOD And A.SCO_MAIN_ROLE = '1' And A.SCO_DT_START <= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_DT_END >= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER)
```

## SSM_H_SAL_DATA!SMCO_HR_ROLE_WORKING_TIME

- TI: `SMCO_HR_ROLE_WORKING_TIME` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_HR_ROLE` → `M4SCO_HR_ROLE`
- Parámetro 1: `SMCO_HR_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 2: `SMCO_HR_OR_PERIOD`
- Parámetro 3: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 4: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 5: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 6: `SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS = C.SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS, @SCO_FULL_TIME = C.SCO_FULL_TIME, @SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD = C.SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD FROM &SCO_HR_ROLE A, SCO_H_HR_ROLE_TIME C WHERE C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND C.SCO_ID_HR(+) = A.SCO_ID_HR AND C.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND (A.SCO_ID_HR = @SMCO_HR_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_OR_HR_PERIOD = @SMCO_HR_OR_PERIOD And A.SCO_DT_START <= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_DT_END >= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_MAIN_ROLE = '1' And C.SCO_DT_START <= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER And C.SCO_DT_END >= @SMCO_DATE_TO_FILTER)
```

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_JOB = A.SCO_ID_JOB, @SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_NM_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_NM_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_GROUP = B.SCO_ID_GROUP, @SCO_NM_LEVEL = D.SCO_NM_LEVEL FROM &SCO_R_JOB_COMP A, SCO_KNOW_MAP B, SCO_KNOW_LEVEL C, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL D WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = B.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_1 = C.SCO_DT_START(+) AND A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = C.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = C.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_LEVEL = D.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND C.SCO_ID_SCALE = D.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND (C.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And #TODAY () <= C.SCO_DT_END And A.SCO_ID_JOB = @SMCO_ID_JOB And A.DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.DT_END >= #TODAY ()) ORDER BY A.SCO_ID_JOB ASC, A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY ASC
```



- Parámetro 1: `SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_DT_START_APP = A.SCO_DT_START_APP, @SCO_NM_APP_STATUS = B.SCO_NM_APP_STATUS, @SCO_GB_NAME = C.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_ID_APP = A.SCO_ID_APP, @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = C.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @STD_N_FIRST_NAME = C.STD_N_FIRST_NAME, @SCO_NM_APP_TYPE = E.SCO_NM_APP_TYPE FROM &SCO_APP_RECRUIT_PR A, SCO_X_APP_STATUS B, STD_PERSON C, SCO_HR_APP D, SCO_X_APP_TYPE E WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_APP_STATUS = B.SCO_ID_APP_STATUS(+) AND A.SCO_ID_APP = C.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_DT_START_APP = D.SCO_DT_START_APP(+) AND A.SCO_ID_APP = D.SCO_ID_APP(+) AND D.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND D.SCO_ID_APP_TYPE = E.SCO_ID_APP_TYPE(+) AND (( #TODAY () >= A.SCO_DT_START And ( #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END Or A.SCO_DT_END IS NULL ) ) And @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR) ORDER BY C.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 ASC, C.STD_N_FIRST_NAME ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_CURRENCY

- TI: `SSM_CURRENCY` · objeto de lectura: `SCH_CURRENCY` → `M4RCH_CURRENCY`

```sql
SELECT @ID_CURRENCY = A.ID_CURRENCY, @NM_CURRENCY = A.NM_CURRENCY FROM &SCH_CURRENCY A ORDER BY A.NM_CURRENCY ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JOB

- TI: `SSM_JOB` · objeto de lectura: `STD_JOB` → `STD_JOB`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @STD_ID_JOB_CODE = A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = A.STD_N_JOB_CODE FROM &STD_JOB A WHERE (A.STD_DT_START <= #TODAY () And ( A.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () Or A.STD_DT_END IS NULL ) And A.STD_ID_JOB_CODE <> 'ROOT') ORDER BY A.STD_N_JOB_CODE ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JOB_POST_ACAD_BACK

- TI: `SSM_JOB_POST_ACAD_BACK` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_JOB_POST_ACAD_BACKGROUND` → `M4SCO_JOB_POST_ACA`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG`
- Parámetro 2: `SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG`

```sql
SELECT @STD_N_DIP_LEVEL = E.STD_N_DIP_LEVEL, @STD_N_EDU_TYPE = H.STD_N_EDU_TYPE, @STD_N_EDU_SP = F.STD_N_EDU_SP, @STD_N_DIPLOMA = B.STD_N_DIPLOMA FROM &SCO_JOB_POST_ACAD_BACKGROUND A, STD_LU_EDU_DIPLOMA B, STD_LU_EDU_DIP_LEVEL E, STD_LU_EDU_SPECIALITY F, STD_LU_EDU_TYPE H WHERE A.STD_ID_DIPLOMA = B.STD_ID_DIPLOMA(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.STD_ID_DIP_LEVEL = E.STD_ID_DIP_LEVEL(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_SP = F.STD_ID_EDU_SP(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE = F.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE(+) AND A.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE = H.STD_ID_EDU_TYPE(+) AND (@SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG = A.SCO_OR_JOB_POST And @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR) ORDER BY A.STD_OR_ACAD_BACK ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JOB_POST_CANC

- TI: `SSM_JOB_POST_CANC` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_JOB_POST` → `M4SSE_JOB_POST`

```sql
SELECT @SSE_OR_JOB_VACANT = A.SSE_OR_JOB_VACANT, @SCO_CHK_MOV_INT = A.SCO_CHK_MOV_INT, @SCO_CHK_MOV_NAC = A.SCO_CHK_MOV_NAC, @SCO_DT_INCORPORATE = A.SCO_DT_INCORPORATE, @SCO_DT_LIMIT = A.SCO_DT_LIMIT, @SCO_ID_WORK_LOCAT = A.SCO_ID_WORK_LOCAT, @SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_MAX_SALARY = A.SCO_MAX_SALARY, @SCO_SALX_CURTYP = A.SCO_SALX_CURTYP, @SCO_SALX_EXCDAT = A.SCO_SALX_EXCDAT, @SCO_SALX_EXCTYP = A.SCO_SALX_EXCTYP, @SCO_MIN_SALARY = A.SCO_MIN_SALARY, @SSE_EST_VAC = A.SSE_EST_VAC, @SSE_NUM_VAC = A.SSE_NUM_VAC, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = B.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_ID_JOB = A.SCO_ID_JOB, @SCO_COMMENT = A.SCO_COMMENT, @SCO_CONSIDERATIONS = A.SCO_CONSIDERATIONS FROM &SSE_JOB_POST A, STD_WORK_UNIT B WHERE A.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = B.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) ORDER BY A.SSE_OR_JOB_VACANT ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JOB_POST_CERT_LIC

- TI: `SSM_JOB_POST_CERT_LIC` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_JOB_POST_CERTIFICATION_LIC` → `M4SCO_JOB_POST_CER`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG`
- Parámetro 2: `SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_N_ISSUE_ENTIT = C.SCO_N_ISSUE_ENTIT, @STD_N_COUNTRY = D.STD_N_COUNTRY, @STD_N_CERTIFICATION_TYPE = B.STD_N_CERTIFICATION_TYPE FROM &SCO_JOB_POST_CERTIFICATION_LIC A, STD_LU_CERTIFICATION_TYPE B, SCO_LU_ISSU_ENT C, STD_COUNTRY D WHERE A.STD_ID_CERTIFICATION_TYPE = B.STD_ID_CERTIFICATION_TYPE(+) AND A.SCO_ID_ISSUE_ENTIT = C.SCO_ID_ISSUE_ENTIT(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = D.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND (@SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG = A.SCO_OR_JOB_POST And @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR) ORDER BY A.DT_START ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JOB_POST_LANG

- TI: `SSM_JOB_POST_LANG_PRO` · objeto de lectura: `STD_JOB_POST_LANG` → `M4STD_JOB_POST_LAN`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG`
- Parámetro 2: `SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG`

```sql
SELECT @STD_N_LANGUAGE = B.STD_N_LANGUAGE, @STD_N_LANG_LEVEL = C.STD_N_LANG_LEVEL, @STD_N_LANG_LEVEL_1 = D.STD_N_LANG_LEVEL, @STD_N_LANG_LEVEL_2 = E.STD_N_LANG_LEVEL FROM &STD_JOB_POST_LANG A, STD_LU_LANGUAGES B, STD_LU_LANG_LEVEL C, STD_LU_LANG_LEVEL D, STD_LU_LANG_LEVEL E WHERE A.STD_ID_LANGUAGE = B.STD_ID_LANGUAGE(+) AND A.STD_ID_READ_LEVEL = C.STD_ID_LANG_LEVEL(+) AND A.STD_ID_SPEAK_LEVEL = D.STD_ID_LANG_LEVEL(+) AND A.STD_ID_WRITE_LEVEL = E.STD_ID_LANG_LEVEL(+) AND (@SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR And @SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG = A.SCO_OR_JOB_POST) ORDER BY A.SCO_CHECK DESC, B.STD_N_LANGUAGE ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JOB_POST_PEND

- TI: `SSM_JOB_POST_PEND` · objeto de lectura: `SSE_JOB_POST` → `M4SSE_JOB_POST`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_JOB = A.SCO_ID_JOB, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = B.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_ID_WORK_LOCAT = A.SCO_ID_WORK_LOCAT, @SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT, @SCO_DT_LIMIT = A.SCO_DT_LIMIT, @SCO_CHK_MOV_INT = A.SCO_CHK_MOV_INT, @SCO_CHK_MOV_NAC = A.SCO_CHK_MOV_NAC, @SSE_OR_JOB_VACANT = A.SSE_OR_JOB_VACANT, @SSE_NUM_VAC = A.SSE_NUM_VAC, @SSE_EST_VAC = A.SSE_EST_VAC, @SCO_MAX_SALARY = A.SCO_MAX_SALARY, @SCO_SALX_CURTYP = A.SCO_SALX_CURTYP, @SCO_SALX_EXCTYP = A.SCO_SALX_EXCTYP, @SCO_MIN_SALARY = A.SCO_MIN_SALARY, @SCO_SALX_EXCDAT = A.SCO_SALX_EXCDAT, @SCO_DT_INCORPORATE = A.SCO_DT_INCORPORATE, @SCO_CONSIDERATIONS = A.SCO_CONSIDERATIONS FROM &SSE_JOB_POST A, STD_WORK_UNIT B WHERE A.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = B.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) ORDER BY A.SSE_OR_JOB_VACANT DESC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JOB_POST_PRO

- TI: `SSM_JOB_POST_PRO` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_JOB_POST` → `M4SCO_JOB_POST`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = A.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT, @STD_N_WORK_UNIT = C.STD_N_WORK_UNIT, @STD_N_WORK_LOCATION = B.STD_N_WORK_LOCATION, @SCO_MIN_SALARY = A.SCO_MIN_SALARY, @SCO_MAX_SALARY = A.SCO_MAX_SALARY, @SCO_SALX_CURTYP = A.SCO_SALX_CURTYP, @SCO_SALX_EXCDAT = A.SCO_SALX_EXCDAT, @SCO_SALX_EXCTYP = A.SCO_SALX_EXCTYP, @SCO_OR_JOB_POST = A.SCO_OR_JOB_POST, @SCO_CONSIDERATIONS = A.SCO_CONSIDERATIONS, @SCO_DT_INCORPORATE = A.SCO_DT_INCORPORATE, @SCO_MAX_AGE = A.SCO_MAX_AGE, @SCO_MIN_AGE = A.SCO_MIN_AGE, @SCO_CHK_MOV_INT = A.SCO_CHK_MOV_INT, @SCO_CHK_MOV_NAC = A.SCO_CHK_MOV_NAC FROM &SCO_JOB_POST A, STD_WORK_LOCATION B, STD_WORK_UNIT C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_WORK_LOCAT = B.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_WORK_UNIT = C.STD_ID_WORK_UNIT(+) AND (( #TODAY () >= A.SCO_DT_START And ( #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END Or A.SCO_DT_END IS NULL ) ) And @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR And A.SCO_ID_HR_DEPEND = $ID_PERSON) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_START ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JP_POST_COMP

- TI: `SSM_JP_POST_COMP` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_R_JOB_POST_COMP` → `M4SCO_JOB_POST_COM`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG`
- Parámetro 2: `SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @SCO_MEANING = C.SCO_MEANING, @SCO_NM_EXTD_KN = B.SCO_NM_EXTD_KN, @SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY, @SCO_WEIGHT = A.SCO_WEIGHT FROM &SCO_R_JOB_POST_COMP A, SCO_KNOW_MAP B, SCO_KNOW_LEVEL C WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_COMPETENCY = B.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = B.ID_ORGANIZATION AND C.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN(+) = B.SCO_ID_EXTD_KN AND (@SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG = A.SCO_OR_JOB_POST And @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR And A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = C.SCO_ID_LEVEL) ORDER BY A.SCO_WEIGHT DESC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_JP_PREV_JOB

- TI: `SSM_JP_PREV_JOB` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_JOB_POST_PREV_JOBS` → `M4SCO_JOB_POST_PRE`
- Parámetro 1: `SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG`
- Parámetro 2: `SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG`

```sql
SELECT @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = B.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_SECTOR = C.STD_N_SECTOR FROM &SCO_JOB_POST_PREV_JOBS A, STD_JOB B, STD_LU_JOB_SECTOR C WHERE A.SCO_ID_PREV_JOB = B.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.STD_ID_SECTOR = C.STD_ID_SECTOR(+) AND (@SCO_OR_JOB_POST_ARG = A.SCO_OR_JOB_POST And @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_ARG = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR) ORDER BY A.DT_START ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_RECRUIT_PRO

- TI: `SSM_RECRUIT_PRO` (raíz) · objeto de lectura: `SCO_RECRUIT_PRO` → `M4SCO_RECRUIT_PRO`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT @SCO_GB_NAME = D.SCO_GB_NAME, @STD_JOB_PATH = E.STD_JOB_PATH, @SCO_DT_REQUEST = A.SCO_DT_REQUEST, @SCO_NM_POSITION = B.SCO_NM_POSITION, @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR_1 = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @SCO_N_AREA = C.SCO_N_AREA, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = E.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1 = D.STD_N_FAMILY_NAME_1, @STD_N_FIRST_NAME = D.STD_N_FIRST_NAME, @SCO_DT_LIMIT = A.SCO_DT_LIMIT, @SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR, @SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT = A.SCO_NM_RECRUITMENT FROM &SCO_RECRUIT_PRO A, SCO_POSITION B, SCO_X_AREA_JOB C, STD_PERSON D, STD_JOB E, SCO_JOB_POST F WHERE F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND F.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR(+) = A.SCO_OR_RECRUIT_PR AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_POSITION = B.SCO_ID_POSITION(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_AREA = C.SCO_ID_AREA(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR_RESPONS = D.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_JOB = E.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND (( #TODAY () >= A.SCO_DT_START And ( #TODAY () <= A.SCO_DT_END Or A.SCO_DT_END IS NULL ) ) And A.SCO_ID_REC_STATUS = '01' And F.SCO_ID_HR_DEPEND = $ID_PERSON) ORDER BY A.SCO_DT_REQUEST ASC
```

## SSM_RECRUIT_PRO!SSM_WORK_LOCATION

- TI: `SSM_WORK_LOCATION` · objeto de lectura: `STD_WORK_LOCATION` → `STD_WORK_LOCATION`

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION = A.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION, @STD_N_WORK_LOCATION = A.STD_N_WORK_LOCATION FROM &STD_WORK_LOCATION A WHERE (#TODAY () >= A.STD_DT_START And ( #TODAY () <= A.STD_DT_END Or A.STD_DT_END IS NULL ) And A.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION <> 'ROOT' And A.STD_ID_WL_TYPE = '1') ORDER BY A.STD_N_WORK_LOCATION ASC
```

```sql
SELECT @HCO_ID_COMP_BASIS = A.HCO_ID_COMP_BASIS, @HCO_DAYS_IN_PERIOD = A.HCO_DAYS_IN_PERIOD FROM &HCO_CR_COMP_BASIS A
```

## SSM_SALARY_REVIEW_PROCESS!SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_DEF

- TI: `SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_DEF` · objeto de lectura: `HCO_CR_COMPA_RATIO` → `M4HCO_CR_COMPA_RTO`
- Parámetro 1: `COMPARATIO_ID_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID = A.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID, @HCO_CR_COMPAR_ORD_ID = A.HCO_CR_COMPAR_ORD_ID, @HCO_CR_MIN_COMPAR = A.HCO_CR_MIN_COMPAR, @HCO_CR_MAX_COMPAR = A.HCO_CR_MAX_COMPAR FROM &HCO_CR_COMPA_RATIO A WHERE (A.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID = @COMPARATIO_ID_FILTER) ORDER BY A.HCO_CR_MIN_COMPAR ASC
```

## SSM_SALARY_REVIEW_PROCESS!SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_LEVELS

- TI: `SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_LEVELS` · objeto de lectura: `HCO_CR_CMPR_DTL` → `M4HCO_CR_CMPR_DTL`
- Conector: `HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID` = `SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_DEF.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID`
- Conector: `HCO_CR_COMPAR_ORD_ID` = `SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_DEF.HCO_CR_COMPAR_ORD_ID`

```sql
SELECT @HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID = A.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID, @HCO_CR_COMPAR_ORD_ID = A.HCO_CR_COMPAR_ORD_ID, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @SCO_ID_SCALE = A.SCO_ID_SCALE, @SCO_ID_LEVEL = A.SCO_ID_LEVEL, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @HCO_CR_MIN_INC_PCT = A.HCO_CR_MIN_INC_PCT, @HCO_CR_MAX_INC_PCT = A.HCO_CR_MAX_INC_PCT, @SCO_NM_LEVEL = B.SCO_NM_LEVEL FROM &HCO_CR_CMPR_DTL A, SCO_SCALE_LEVEL B WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_LEVEL = B.SCO_ID_LEVEL(+) AND A.SCO_ID_SCALE = B.SCO_ID_SCALE(+) AND (A.DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.DT_END >= #TODAY () And A.SCO_ID_SCALE = 'GLOBAL_CC')
```

## SSM_SALARY_REVIEW_PROCESS!SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_MTH

- TI: `SSM_COMPARATIO_INCREASE_MTH` · objeto de lectura: `HCO_CR_COMPR_INC` → `MHCO_CR_COMPR_INC`
- Parámetro 1: `COMPARATIO_ID_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID = A.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID, @HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_NM = A.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_NM, @HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_DESC = A.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_DESC FROM &HCO_CR_COMPR_INC A WHERE (A.HCO_CR_CMPR_INC_ID = @COMPARATIO_ID_FILTER)
```

## SSM_SALARY_REVIEW_PROCESS!SSM_CR_H_HR_SAL_REV_EMP

- TI: `SHCO_CR_H_HR_SAL_REV_EMP` · objeto de lectura: `HCO_CR_H_HR_SAL_REV` → `M4HCO_CR_H_HR_SREV`
- Parámetro 1: `EMPLOYEE_ID`
- Parámetro 2: `EMPLOYEE_ROLE`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_N_ROLE = B.SCO_N_ROLE, @HCO_CR_SALARY_P_NM = E.HCO_CR_SALARY_P_NM, @SCO_GB_NAME = D.SCO_GB_NAME, @HCO_CR_SPLAN_TP_NM = F.HCO_CR_SPLAN_TP_NM, @SCO_GB_NAME_MANAGER = G.SCO_GB_NAME, @STD_ID_HR_MANAGER = A.STD_ID_HR_MANAGER, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID = A.HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @HCO_CR_SAL_STRC_ID = A.HCO_CR_SAL_STRC_ID, @HCO_CR_SAL_REV_VALUE = A.HCO_CR_SAL_REV_VALUE, @ID_CURRENCY = A.ID_CURRENCY, @CUR_DATE = A.CUR_DATE, @EX_TYPE = A.EX_TYPE, @HCO_CR_INC_PERCENTAGE = A.HCO_CR_INC_PERCENTAGE, @HCO_CR_REVIEW_DATE = A.HCO_CR_REVIEW_DATE FROM &HCO_CR_H_HR_SAL_REV A, SCO_HR_ROLE B, STD_HR C, STD_PERSON D, SLCO_VW_CR_SALARY_PLAN E, HCO_CR_SAL_PLAN_TP F, STD_PERSON G WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = B.SCO_ID_HR(+) AND A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = B.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_HR = C.STD_ID_HR(+) AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.STD_ID_HR = D.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND A.HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID = E.HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND E.HCO_CR_SPLAN_TP_ID = F.HCO_CR_SPLAN_TP_ID(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_HR_MANAGER = G.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (A.SCO_ID_HR = @EMPLOYEE_ID And A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @EMPLOYEE_ROLE And ( A.HCO_CR_REVIEW_STATE IS NULL Or A.HCO_CR_REVIEW_STATE = 'ACEPTAR' ))
```

## SSM_SALARY_REVIEW_PROCESS!SSM_EMPLOYEE_PARTIAL_TIME

- TI: `SSM_EMPLOYEE_PARTIAL_TIME` · objeto de lectura: `SCO_H_HR_ROLE_TIME` → `M4SCO_H_HR_RO_TIME`

- Parámetro 2: `EMPLOYEE_ROLE_FOR_FILTER`
- Parámetro 3: `DATE_TO_FILTER`
- Parámetro 4: `DATE_TO_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS = A.SCO_ROLE_WEEKLY_HOURS, @SCO_FULL_TIME = A.SCO_FULL_TIME, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD = A.SCO_PERCENT_PERIOD FROM &SCO_H_HR_ROLE_TIME A WHERE (A.SCO_ID_HR = @EMPLOYEE_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @EMPLOYEE_ROLE_FOR_FILTER And A.SCO_DT_START <= @DATE_TO_FILTER And A.SCO_DT_END >= @DATE_TO_FILTER)
```

## SSM_SALARY_REVIEW_PROCESS!SSM_EMPLOYEE_SALARY_PLANS

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = C.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE, @STD_N_JOB_CODE = D.STD_N_JOB_CODE, @SCO_N_ROLE = A.SCO_N_ROLE, @SCO_GB_NAME = F.SCO_GB_NAME, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE FROM &SCO_HR_ROLE A, SCO_H_HR_POSITION B, SCO_POSITION C, STD_JOB D, STD_HR E, STD_PERSON F, SCO_H_POSITION G WHERE B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = A.ID_ORGANIZATION AND B.SCO_ID_HR(+) = A.SCO_ID_HR AND B.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE(+) = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE AND B.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND B.SCO_ID_POSITION = C.SCO_ID_POSITION(+) AND G.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) = C.ID_ORGANIZATION AND G.SCO_ID_POSITION(+) = C.SCO_ID_POSITION AND C.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND C.SCO_ID_JOB_CODE = D.STD_ID_JOB_CODE(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.SCO_ID_HR = E.STD_ID_HR(+) AND E.ID_ORGANIZATION = F.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND E.STD_ID_HR = F.STD_ID_PERSON(+) AND (( A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () ) And ( B.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And B.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () ) And ( C.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And C.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () ) And ( G.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And G.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () ) And ( D.STD_DT_START <= #TODAY () And D.STD_DT_END >= #TODAY () ))
```

## SSM_SALARY_REVIEW_PROCESS!SSM_SAL_REV_HT_DEL_RECORDS

- TI: `SSM_SAL_REV_HT_DEL_RECORDS` · objeto de lectura: `HCO_CR_H_HR_SAL_REV` → `M4HCO_CR_H_HR_SREV`
- Parámetro 1: `ID_SAL_TP_PLAN_FILTER`
- Parámetro 2: `EMPLOYEE_FILTER`
- Parámetro 3: `EMPLOYEE_ROLE_FILTER`

```sql
SELECT @SCO_ID_HR = A.SCO_ID_HR, @HCO_CR_SAL_REV_VALUE = A.HCO_CR_SAL_REV_VALUE, @ID_CURRENCY = A.ID_CURRENCY, @CUR_DATE = A.CUR_DATE, @HCO_CR_VALUE_TABLE = B.HCO_CR_VALUE_TABLE, @EX_TYPE = A.EX_TYPE, @ID_DMD = B.ID_DMD, @ID_DMD_COMPONENT = B.ID_DMD_COMPONENT, @HCO_CR_SPLAN_TP_ID = B.HCO_CR_SPLAN_TP_ID, @SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE, @DT_START = A.DT_START, @DT_END = A.DT_END, @HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID = A.HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID FROM &HCO_CR_H_HR_SAL_REV A, SLCO_VW_CR_SALARY_PLAN B WHERE A.HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID = B.HCO_CR_SALARY_P_ID(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND (B.HCO_CR_SPLAN_TP_ID = @ID_SAL_TP_PLAN_FILTER And A.SCO_ID_HR = @EMPLOYEE_FILTER And A.SCO_OR_HR_ROLE = @EMPLOYEE_ROLE_FILTER) ORDER BY A.DT_START DESC
```

```sql
SELECT @STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION = A.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION, @STD_N_GEO_PLACE = E.STD_N_GEO_PLACE, @SCO_CHK_MAIN_ADDRESS = A.SCO_CHK_MAIN_ADDRESS, @SCO_OR_WL_ADDRESS = A.SCO_OR_WL_ADDRESS, @SCO_DT_START = A.SCO_DT_START, @SCO_DT_END = A.SCO_DT_END, @STD_N_COUNTRY = B.STD_N_COUNTRY, @STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV = C.STD_N_SUB_GEO_DIV, @STD_N_GEO_DIV = D.STD_N_GEO_DIV, @SCO_ZIP_CODE = A.SCO_ZIP_CODE, @STD_ID_COUNTRY = A.STD_ID_COUNTRY, @STD_ID_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV, @STD_ID_GEO_PLACE = A.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE, @STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV, @SCO_GB_ADDRESS = A.SCO_GB_ADDRESS FROM &SCO_H_WLOCATION_ADDRESS A, STD_GEO_DIV D, STD_SUB_GEO_DIV C, STD_COUNTRY B, STD_GEO_PLACE E WHERE A.ID_ORGANIZATION = D.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = D.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = D.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = C.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = C.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = C.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = C.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = B.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = B.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.ID_ORGANIZATION = E.ID_ORGANIZATION(+) AND A.STD_ID_COUNTRY = E.STD_ID_COUNTRY(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_DIV = E.STD_ID_GEO_DIV(+) AND A.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE = E.STD_ID_GEO_PLACE(+) AND A.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV = E.STD_ID_SUB_GEO_DIV(+) AND (A.SCO_DT_START <= #TODAY () And A.SCO_DT_END >= #TODAY () And A.STD_ID_WORK_LOCATION = @WORK_LOCATION And A.SCO_CHK_MAIN_ADDRESS = '1')
```

